	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu5+4, solmargin+247, satiryuksekligi*3, 20, "8-Diðer")
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(13,7)
	
	Grid(0,0) = "DM"
	Grid(1,0) = "VIDA"
	Grid(2,0) = "_FORMUL"
	Grid(3,0) = "KAYNAK_AD"
	Grid(4,0) = "R_KAYNAKKODU"
	Grid(5,0) = "RECETENOTU"
	Grid(6,0) = "R_MIKTART"
	Grid(7,0) = ""
	Grid(8,0) = ""
	Grid(9,0) = "_ISI_DAYANIM"
	Grid(10,0) = "_ESKI_HMD"
	Grid(11,0) = "_ESKI_FORMUL"
	Grid(12,0) = "_ESKI_VIDA"
	Grid(13,0) = "_ESKI_DOZAJ"
	
	Grid(0,1) = 10
	Grid(1,1) = 13
	Grid(2,1) = 18
	Grid(3,1) = 61
	Grid(4,1) = 26
	Grid(5,1) = 22
	Grid(6,1) = 18
	Grid(7,1) = 15
	Grid(8,1) = 15
	Grid(9,1) = 14
	Grid(10,1) = 28
	Grid(11,1) = 12
	Grid(12,1) = 11
	Grid(13,1) = 10
	
	Grid(0,2) = "DZJ. MT."
	Grid(1,2) = "VÝDA TÝPÝ"
	Grid(2,2) = "FORMÜL"
	Grid(3,2) = "HAMMADDE ADI"
	Grid(4,2) = "HAMMADDE KODU"
	Grid(5,2) = "HAMMADDE PARTÝ NO"
	Grid(6,2) = "MÝKTAR"
	Grid(7,2) = "ÝADE"
	Grid(8,2) = "ÝLAVE"
	Grid(9,2) = "HMD.DY. IS.°C"
	Grid(10,2) = "Hammade Adý"
	Grid(11,2) = "Formül" & vbcrlf & "%"
	Grid(12,2) = "Vida Tipi"
	Grid(13,2) = "Dzj. Mt."
	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "N"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "C"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "N"
	Grid(7,4) = "C"
	Grid(8,4) = "N"
	Grid(9,4) = "N"
	Grid(10,4) = "C"
	Grid(11,4) = "N"
	Grid(12,4) = "C"
	Grid(13,4) = "C"
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
			Grid(i,6) = MMPS10T.MaxSigDig(AlanAdi)
		End If
	Next
End Sub
Function Basilacak()
	Basilacak = False
	Set M10TH1 = Doc.Select2G("fw4fs", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'", "", "EVRAKNO", -1)
	if M10TH1.GetRowCount = 0 Then Exit Function
	
	if Doc.Round(M10TH1.R_MIKTART(1), 3) = Doc.Round(MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, 3) Then Basilacak = True
End Function

Public sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic, SatirBitis
Public AlanSayisi(), ToplamTutar, ToplamProTutar, Kod, ondalik1, toplamkg, formulkg, Std
Public xCizgi()
Public Secilenler()
Public Grid()

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 280
	UstMargin = 5
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 10 
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 4.3
	satirBaslangic = 50
	satirsonu=satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*7
	toplamkg=0
	formulkg=0
	std=""
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()


	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satinalma Siparisi",SayfaEni,SayfaBoyu,"LANDSCAPE")

	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If

	satirSayisi = MMPS10T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.SetDevPaperFormat(-2)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)

	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",9)
	res =pe1.setbold(1)
	ReDim Preserve xCizgi(0)
		sol=solmargin

		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 17, Sol, 15, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)) = Sol - 2
				ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
		Next

	res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 17, Solmargin+230, 15, 40 , "LABORATUVAR AÇIKLAMA")
	GidaUygunluk = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "BI_FOOD_AP", 0)
	if trim(GidaUygunluk) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 16)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 9, Solmargin+229, 30, 30 , "GIDAYA UYGUNLUK : " & trim(GidaUygunluk))
		res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	End if
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",10)
	res=pe1.write(ustmargin+20, solmargin+90, satiryuksekligi, 70, "ÜRÜN REÇETE BÝLGÝSÝ")

	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",9)
	kod=MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(1)
	kodad=MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD(1)
	musteri=MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
	musterad=MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD(1)
	sipno=MMPS10E.MUSTERISIPINO(1)
	sipartno=MMPS10E.MUSTERISIPIartNO(1)
	sipmiktar=doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK40T", "SF_MIKTAR", sipartno, "ARTNO")
	testarih=MMPS10E.URETIMDENTESTARIH(1)
	mpsno=MMPS10E.EVRAKNO(1)
	miktar=MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(1)
	not1=MMPS10E.NOTLAR_1(1)
	not2=MMPS10E.NOTLAR_2(1)
	parti= MMPS10E.NOTLAR_1

	rows = Doc.SelectEQ("BOMU01E", "MAMULCODE", kod) 
	Set Old = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_BOMU01E_SELECT")
 	For i=1 to rows 
	
	aktif = Old.AKTIF_PASIF(i)
		if aktif="*" then
			kons = Old.ACIKLAMA(i)
			evno=old.EVRAKNO(i)
			std= doc.GetUDFValue("BOMU01E",evno,"STD",0)

 		End if
	 Next 

	
'------------baslýklar-1---------
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	baslangic=ustmargin+5
	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",9)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res=pe1.selectfont("courier new",9)


	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res=pe1.write(baslangic, solmargin,  satiryuksekligi, 45,"ÜRÜN ADI")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic, solmargin+25,  satiryuksekligi, 45,":" & trim(kod)& " " & Trim(kodad))
	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",9)

	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi, solmargin,  satiryuksekligi, 45,"MÜÞTERÝ ADI")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+25,  satiryuksekligi, 45,":" & trim(musterad))
	res=pe1.selectfont("courier new",9)
	
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi*2, solmargin, satiryuksekligi, 45, "PARTÝ NO")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi*2, solmargin+25,  satiryuksekligi, 45," " & trim(parti))	
	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",9)

	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi*3, solmargin, satiryuksekligi, 45, "ÜRETÝLECEK MÝKTAR")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi*3, solmargin+35,  satiryuksekligi, 45,":" & doc.numformat_nlz(miktar,12,2))
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi*3, solmargin+140, satiryuksekligi, 45, "SÝPARÝÞ MÝKTAR")

	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi*3, solmargin+170,  satiryuksekligi, 45,":" & doc.numformat_nlz(sipmiktar,12,2))

	res=pe1.write(baslangic, solmargin+75,  satiryuksekligi, 45,"KONSANTRASYON")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic, solmargin+105,  satiryuksekligi, 45,":" & trim(kons))
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+75,  satiryuksekligi, 45,"SÝPARÝÞ NO")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+105,  satiryuksekligi, 45,":" & trim(sipno))
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi*2, solmargin+75, satiryuksekligi, 45, "STANDART NO")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi*2, solmargin+105,  satiryuksekligi, 45,":" & trim(std))
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	

	res=pe1.write(baslangic, solmargin+140,  satiryuksekligi, 45,"TESLÝM TARÝHÝ")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic, solmargin+165,  satiryuksekligi, 45,":" & doc.strtonordate(testarih))
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+140, satiryuksekligi, 45, "MAKÝNA ADI")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+165,  satiryuksekligi, 45,":")

	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",10)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res=pe1.write(baslangic, solmargin+200,  satiryuksekligi, 45,"MPS ")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic, solmargin+210,  satiryuksekligi, 45,":" & trim(mpsno))
	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",9)
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+200,  satiryuksekligi, 45,"ÜRT.BAÞ.SAATÝ")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi*2, solmargin+200,  satiryuksekligi, 45,"ÜRT.BAÞ./BÝT.SAATÝ")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi*3, solmargin+200, satiryuksekligi, 45, "ÜRT.BÝT.SAATÝ")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi*4, solmargin+200, satiryuksekligi, 45, "Form No ")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi*4, solmargin+235,  satiryuksekligi, 15,": 39")




End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",9)

Dim satirpozr
		sol=solmargin
		If trim(MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(satirNo)) = "I" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
		If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then

			Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic-8.6
		Else
			If satirNo = 1 Then
					satirpozr = satirBaslangic-8.6
			Else
					satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi

			End If
		End If		
		hammaddekod=MMPS10T.TRNUM(satirno)
			hammaddekod=MMPS10T.SRNUM(satirno)
			hammaddekod1=MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirno)

			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				if degiskenadi="?" then
					degisken=""
				Elseif degiskenadi="!" then
				
	kod=MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(1)
					rows1 = Doc.SelectEQ("BOMU01T", "BOMREC_CODE", kod) 
					Set Old1 = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_BOMU01T_SELECT")
 					For x=1 To rows1
						If old1.AKTIF_PASIF(x)="*" And Trim(old1.TRNUM(x))=Trim(hammaddekod) and Trim(old1.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(x))=Trim(hammaddekod1) then
						degisken=old1.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(x)
						ondalik1=old1.MaxSigDig("BOMREC_KAYNAK0")	
						formulkg=formulkg+degisken
					End If
					Next

				elseif degiskenadi="DM" then
					kaynakkodu=MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirno)
					DM = doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10T",trim(jobno),"DM",0)

					degisken=trim(DM)
				elseif degiskenadi="VIDA" then
					kaynakkodu=MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirno)
					vida=doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10T",trim(jobno),"VIDA",0)
					degisken=trim(vida)
				Else
				Degisken = trim(cstr(MMPS10T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
				end if
				if degiskenadi="KAYNAK_AD" then
						if degisken="" then
						degisken=MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirno)
						end if
				end if		

				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					if degiskenadi="!" then
					ondalik=2
					else
					Ondalik = 3
					end if
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					degisken=doc.numformat_NLZ(degisken, grid(i,1), ondalik)
				ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
					Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
				End If
				if degiskenadi="R_MIKTART" then
				toplamkg=toplamkg+degisken
				end if
			
				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
			
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
				Next
				
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.setbold(1)

	res=pe1.write(satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*7-2, solmargin+15, satiryuksekligi, 20, "TOPLAM :")
	res=pe1.write(satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*7-2, solmargin+38, satiryuksekligi, 20,doc.numformat(formulkg,12,2))
	res=pe1.write(satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*7-2, solmargin+165, satiryuksekligi, 70,doc.numformat(toplamkg,12,3))



	son2=satirsonu+satiryuksekligi*4

'DÝKEY ÇÝZGÝLER (SUTUN ÇÝZGÝLERÝ)
		For i = 0 To UBound(grid,1) -2
	  		LineX = cint(xCizgi(i))
			If LineX = 0 Then Exit For
	 		res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 18, LineX, 44, 0)
		Next

'-------satýrlar için cerceve ve cizgiler
 	'res = pe1.box(satirBaslangic - 14, solmargin-2, son2-satirbaslangic+8, 230)
 	res = pe1.box(satirBaslangic - 18, solmargin-2, 44, 225)

	for i=0 to 7
	 		res = pe1.line((satirBaslangic-9)+i*satiryuksekligi, solmargin-2, 0, 225)
	next

	res = pe1.box(satirBaslangic - 18, solmargin+228, 44, 33)
	res = pe1.line(satirBaslangic-9,solmargin+228,0,33)

	
		res=pe1.setalign("LEFT")
'---------SICAKLIKLAR
	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",9)
	satirsonu1=satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*8

	res = pe1.box(satirsonu1, solmargin-2, 21, 210)
	res = pe1.box(satirsonu1, solmargin+213, 50, 48)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin-2, 0, 210)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+13, solmargin-2, 0, 210)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+9, solmargin+213, 0, 48)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+40, solmargin+213, 0, 48)

	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+7, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+15, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+24, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+34, 16, 0)	
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+44, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+55, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+66, 16, 0)	
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+76, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+87, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1, solmargin+97, 21, 0)	
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+123, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+150, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+170, 16, 0)	
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+188, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)

		res=pe1.write(satirsonu5, solmargin+152, satiryuksekligi*3, 60, "6-Operatör eksikliði")
		res=pe1.write(satirsonu5, solmargin+180, satiryuksekligi*3, 25, "7-Ön kontrol")
		res=pe1.write(satirsonu5, solmargin+203, satiryuksekligi*3, 25, "8-HMD tartýmý")
		res=pe1.write(satirsonu5, solmargin+229, satiryuksekligi*3, 25, "9-Sipariþ yok")
		res=pe1.write(satirsonu5, solmargin+247, satiryuksekligi*3, 20, "10-Diðer")


		res=pe1.write(satirsonu5+4, solmargin-2, satiryuksekligi*3, 60, "Fire sebep ve kodlarý")
		res=pe1.write(satirsonu5+4, solmargin+29, satiryuksekligi*3, 60, "1-HMD nemli")
		res=pe1.write(satirsonu5+4, solmargin+54, satiryuksekligi*3, 60, "2-Dozajlama üni. kaynaklý fire")		
		res=pe1.write(satirsonu5+4, solmargin+94, satiryuksekligi*3, 60, "3-Extruder kaynaklý fire")		
		res=pe1.write(satirsonu5+4, solmargin+126, satiryuksekligi*3, 60, "4-Kesim kaynaklý fire")		
		res=pe1.write(satirsonu5+4, solmargin+152, satiryuksekligi*3, 35, "5-spagetti kopmasý")	
		res=pe1.write(satirsonu5+4, solmargin+180, satiryuksekligi*3, 40, "6-dur-kalk kaynaklý fire")
		res=pe1.write(satirsonu5+4, solmargin+214, satiryuksekligi*3, 35, "7-Dolum kaynaklý fire")	
		res=pe1.write(satirsonu5+4, solmargin+247, satiryuksekligi*3, 20, "8-Diðer")	


		
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(9,7)

	Grid(0,0) = "DM"
	Grid(1,0) = "VIDA"
	Grid(2,0) = "!"
	Grid(3,0) = "KAYNAK_AD"
	Grid(4,0) = "R_KAYNAKKODU"
	Grid(5,0) = "?"
	Grid(6,0) = "R_MIKTART"
	Grid(7,0) = ""
	Grid(8,0) = ""
	
	Grid(0,1) = 18
	Grid(1,1) = 18
	Grid(2,1) = 18
	Grid(3,1) = 55
	Grid(4,1) = 30
	Grid(5,1) = 30
	Grid(6,1) = 18
	Grid(7,1) = 15
	Grid(8,1) = 23

	Grid(0,2) = "DOZAJ. MOTOR"
	Grid(1,2) = "VÝDA TÝPÝ"
	Grid(2,2) = "FORMUL"
	Grid(3,2) = "HAMMADDE ADI"
	Grid(4,2) = "HAMMADDE KODU"
	Grid(5,2) = "HAMMADDE PARTI NO"
	Grid(6,2) = "MIKTAR"
	Grid(7,2) = "ÝADE"
	Grid(8,2) = "HAMMADDE DYN.ISISI C"

	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "N"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "C"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "N"
	Grid(7,4) = "C"
	Grid(8,4) = "C"
		
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			
			ondalik = MMPS10T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
			
		End If

	Next	
	For i = 0 To uBound(Grid,1) - 1
		ToplamEn = ToplamEn + Grid(i,1)
	Next
End Sub                                                                                                          


----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SENK_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : ZMPS13
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : ÜRETÝM TAKÝP FORMU / - COR
Kod : 
Public sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic, SatirBitis, M10T, toplammiktar
Public Kod, ondalik1, toplamkg, formulkg, Std
Public xCizgi(), Grid(), solFormul, GenFormul, solToplam, GenToplam

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	UstMargin = 5
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 10
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 5
	satirBaslangic = 50
	satirsonu=satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*7
	toplamkg=0
	formulkg=0
	std=""
	TOPLAMMiktar=0
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	
	
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satinalma Siparisi",SayfaEni,SayfaBoyu,"LANDSCAPE")
	
	Call Alanlar()
	
	satirSayisi = MMPS10T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	
	Doc.CreateTableObject "XXX=MMPS10T", "TMPCOPY"
	Set M10T = Doc.GetTableObject("XXX")
	For m=1 to satirsayisi
		if trim(MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(m))="I" and trim(MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON(m))="COR" then
			miktart=MMPS10T.R_YMK_YMPAKETICERIGI(m)
			miktar0=MMPS10T.R_YMK_YMPAKETICERIGI(m)			
		end if
		if trim(MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(m))="H" and trim(MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON(m))="COR" and MMPS10T.TRANSFERPARTIBUYUKLG(m)>0 Then
			miktar_h=miktar_h+MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR0(m)
		end if
	next
	miktar0=miktar0 - miktar_h
	M10T.AddRow
	satirNo1 = M10T.GetRecCount
	M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirno1)=MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU
	M10T.KAYNAK_AD(satirno1)=doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK00", "AD", MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "")
	M10T.R_MIKTAR0(satirno1)=miktar0
	M10T.R_MIKTART(satirno1)=miktar0
	M10T.TRNUM(satirno1)="XXXXXX"
	M10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(satirno1)="H"
	M10T.TRANSFERPARTIBUYUKLG(satirno1)="10"
	for k=1 to satirsayisi
		if trim(MMPS10T.R_OPERASYON(k))="COR" then
			M10T.AddRow
			satirNo1 = M10T.GetRecCount
			M10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(satirno1)=MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(k)
			M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirno1)=MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(k)
			M10T.KAYNAK_AD(satirno1)=MMPS10T.KAYNAK_AD(k)
			M10T.RECETENOTU(satirno1)=MMPS10T.RECETENOTU(k)
			M10T.R_MIKTAR0(satirno1)=MMPS10T.R_MIKTAR0(k)
			M10T.R_MIKTART(satirno1)=MMPS10T.R_MIKTART(k)
			M10T.TRNUM(satirno1)=MMPS10T.TRNUM(k)
			M10T.TRANSFERPARTIBUYUKLG(satirno1)=MMPS10T.TRANSFERPARTIBUYUKLG(k)			
		end if
	next
	for n=1 to M10T.getreccount()
		if trim(M10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(n))="H" and M10T.TRANSFERPARTIBUYUKLG(n)>0 Then
			toplammiktar=toplammiktar+M10T.R_MIKTAR0(n)
		end if	
	Next
	For i = 1 To M10T.getreccount()
		if trim(M10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(i))="H" Then
			Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
		end if
	Next
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",9)
	res =pe1.setbold(1)
	ReDim Preserve xCizgi(0)
	sol=solmargin
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 13, Sol, 15, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)) = Sol - 2
		ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
	Next
	
	res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 13, Solmargin+240, 15, 40 , "LABORATUVAR AÇIKLAMA")
	GidaUygunluk = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "BI_FOOD_AP", 0)
	if trim(GidaUygunluk) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 16)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 5, Solmargin+229, 30, 30 , "GIDAYA UYGUNLUK : " & trim(GidaUygunluk))
		res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	End if
	
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",11)
	res=pe1.write(ustmargin+25, solmargin+90, satiryuksekligi, 70, "ÜRÜN REÇETE BÝLGÝSÝ")
	
	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",9)
	kod=MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(1)
	kodad=MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD(1)
	musteri=MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
	musterad=MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD(1)
	sipno=MMPS10E.MUSTERISIPINO(1)
	testarih=MMPS10E.URETIMDENTESTARIH(1)
	mpsno=MMPS10E.EVRAKNO(1)
	miktar=MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(1)
	not1=MMPS10E.NOTLAR_1(1)
	not2=MMPS10E.NOTLAR_2(1)
	parti= MMPS10E.NOTLAR_1
	
	rows = Doc.SelectEQ("BOMU01E", "MAMULCODE", kod)
	Set Old = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_BOMU01E_SELECT")
	For i=1 to rows	
		aktif = Old.AKTIF_PASIF(i)
		if aktif="*" then
			kons = Old.ACIKLAMA(i)
			evno=old.EVRAKNO(i)
			std= doc.GetUDFValue("BOMU01E",evno,"STD",0)
		End if
	Next
	
	'------------baslýklar-1---------
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	baslangic=ustmargin+5
	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",9)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	
	res=pe1.write(baslangic, solmargin,  satiryuksekligi, 45,"ÜRÜN ADI")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic, solmargin+25,  satiryuksekligi, 45,":" & trim(kod)& " " & Trim(kodad))
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi, solmargin,  satiryuksekligi, 45,"MÜÞTERÝ ADI")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+25,  satiryuksekligi, 45,":" & trim(musterad))
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi*2, solmargin, satiryuksekligi, 45, "PARTÝ NO")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi*2, solmargin+25,  satiryuksekligi, 45,":" & trim(parti))
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi*3, solmargin, satiryuksekligi, 45, "ÜRETÝLECEK MÝKTAR")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi*3, solmargin+35,  satiryuksekligi, 45,":" & doc.numformat_nlz(miktar,12,2))
	
	res=pe1.write(baslangic, solmargin+75,  satiryuksekligi, 45,"KONSANTRASYON")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic, solmargin+105,  satiryuksekligi, 45,":" & trim(kons))
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+75,  satiryuksekligi, 45,"SÝPARÝÞ NO")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+105,  satiryuksekligi, 45,":" & trim(sipno))
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi*2, solmargin+75, satiryuksekligi, 45, "STANDART NO")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi*2, solmargin+105,  satiryuksekligi, 45,":" & trim(std))
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	
	
	res=pe1.write(baslangic, solmargin+140,  satiryuksekligi, 45,"TESLÝM TARÝHÝ")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic, solmargin+165,  satiryuksekligi, 45,":" & doc.strtonordate(testarih))
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+140, satiryuksekligi, 45, "MAKÝNA ADI")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi, solmargin+165,  satiryuksekligi, 45,":")
	
	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",12)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res=pe1.write(baslangic, solmargin+200,  satiryuksekligi, 45,"MPS")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic, solmargin+210,  satiryuksekligi, 45,":" & trim(mpsno))
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",9)
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi+2, solmargin+200,  satiryuksekligi, 45,"ÜRT.BAÞ.SAATÝ")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi*2+2, solmargin+200, satiryuksekligi, 45, "ÜRT.BÝT.SAATÝ")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi*3+2, solmargin+200, satiryuksekligi, 45, "Form No ")
	res=pe1.write(baslangic+satiryuksekligi*3+2, solmargin+235,  satiryuksekligi, 45,": 39")
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
	
	Dim satirpozr
	sol=solmargin
	If trim(M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
	Degisken = ""
	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then		
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic-4
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic-4
		Else
			satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
			
		End If
	End If
	hammaddekod=M10T.TRNUM(satirno)
	hammaddekod1=M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirno)
	For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		res=pe1.selectfont("arial",9)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
		
		if degiskenadi="?" then
			degisken=""
		elseif degiskenadi="!" then
			if M10T.TRANSFERPARTIBUYUKLG(satirno)>0 then
				
				degisken=doc.div(M10T.R_MIKTAR0(satirno) , toplammiktar)* 100
				formulkg=formulkg+degisken
				res = pe1.SelectFont("Lucida Console", 12)
				
			end if
			solFormul = sol
			GenFormul = sutunGenisligi
		elseif degiskenadi="_ISI_DAYANIM" Then
			Degisken = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "B_B_TECH_5", M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo), "")
		else
			Degisken = M10T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)
		end if
		if degiskenadi="KAYNAK_AD" then
			if degisken="" then
				degisken=M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirno)
			end if
		end if
		if degiskenadi="R_MIKTART" then
			toplamkg=toplamkg+degisken
			solToplam = sol
			GenToplam = sutunGenisligi
			res = pe1.SelectFont("Lucida Console", 10)
		end if
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			if degiskenadi="!" then
				ondalik=2
			else
				Ondalik = 3
			end if
			degisken=doc.numformat_NLZ(degisken, grid(i,1), ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
		End If
		
		
		res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)
		
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next
	
	
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res=pe1.write(satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*7+1, solmargin, satiryuksekligi, 20, "TOPLAM :")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res=pe1.write(satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*7+1, solFormul, satiryuksekligi, GenFormul - 2,doc.numformat(formulkg,12,2))
	res=pe1.write(satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*7+1, solToplam, satiryuksekligi, GenToplam - 2,doc.numformat(toplamkg,12,3))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	
	
	son2=satirsonu+satiryuksekligi*4
	
	'DÝKEY ÇÝZGÝLER (SUTUN ÇÝZGÝLERÝ)
	For i = 0 To UBound(grid,1) - 1
		LineX = cint(xCizgi(i))
		If LineX = 0 Then Exit For
		res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 14, LineX, 49, 0)
	Next
	
	'-------satýrlar için cerceve ve cizgiler
	'res = pe1.box(satirBaslangic - 14, solmargin-2, son2-satirbaslangic+8, 230)
	res = pe1.box(satirBaslangic - 14, solmargin-2, 49, 228)
	
	for i=0 to 7
		res = pe1.line((satirBaslangic-4.5) + i*satiryuksekligi, solmargin-2, 0, 228)
	next
	
	res = pe1.box(satirBaslangic - 14, solmargin+228, 49, 43)
	res = pe1.line(satirBaslangic-5,solmargin+228,0,43)
	
	
	res=pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	'---------SICAKLIKLAR
	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",9)
	satirsonu1=satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*8
	
	res = pe1.box(satirsonu1, solmargin-2, 21, 210)
	res = pe1.box(satirsonu1, solmargin+213, 50, 58)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin-2, 0, 210)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+13, solmargin-2, 0, 210)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+13, solmargin+213, 0, 58)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+40, solmargin+213, 0, 58)
	
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+7, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+15, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+24, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+34, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+44, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+55, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+66, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+76, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+87, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1, solmargin+97, 21, 0)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+123, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+150, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+170, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+188, 16, 0)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	
	res=pe1.write(satirsonu1+1, solmargin+30, satiryuksekligi, 70, "SICAKLIK BÝLGÝLERÝ")
	res=pe1.write(satirsonu1+1, solmargin+140, satiryuksekligi, 70, "PROSES BÝLGÝLERÝ")
	res=pe1.write(satirsonu1+5, solmargin+228, satiryuksekligi, 40, "ÜRETÝM AÇIKLAMA")
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	
	kod=MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(1)
	rows = Doc.SelectEQ("BOMU01E", "MAMULCODE", kod)
	Set Old = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_BOMU01E_SELECT")
	For i=1 to rows
		
		aktif = Old.AKTIF_PASIF(i)
		if aktif="*" then
			kons = Old.ACIKLAMA(i)
			evno=old.EVRAKNO(i)
			std= doc.GetUDFValue("BOMU01E",evno,"STD",0)
			
		End if
	Next
	
	if trim(std)="" then
		res=pe1.write(satirsonu1+43, solmargin+215, satiryuksekligi, 45,"Numune Miktar : 0.5 kg")
	else
		res=pe1.write(satirsonu1+43, solmargin+215, satiryuksekligi, 45,"Numune Miktar : 0.15 kg")
		
	end if
	
	stokkodu=MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(1)
	oner1= doc.Getdbfieldvalue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_11",stokkodu,"")
	oner2= doc.Getdbfieldvalue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_12",stokkodu,"")
	oner3= doc.Getdbfieldvalue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_13",stokkodu,"")
	oner4= doc.Getdbfieldvalue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_14",stokkodu,"")
	oner5= doc.Getdbfieldvalue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_15",stokkodu,"")
	oner6= doc.Getdbfieldvalue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_16",stokkodu,"")
	oner7= doc.Getdbfieldvalue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_17",stokkodu,"")
	oner8= doc.Getdbfieldvalue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_18",stokkodu,"")
	oner9= doc.Getdbfieldvalue("STOK00","B_B_TECH_19",stokkodu,"")
	
	B=solmargin+5
	s=10
	res=pe1.write(satirsonu1+satiryuksekligi*1.5, b-6, satiryuksekligi, 10, "ÖNR")
	res=pe1.write(satirsonu1+satiryuksekligi*1.5, b+5, satiryuksekligi, 10, oner1)
	res=pe1.write(satirsonu1+satiryuksekligi*1.5, b+14, satiryuksekligi, 10,oner2)

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(9,7)
	
	Grid(0,0) = "?"
	Grid(1,0) = "!"
	Grid(2,0) = "KAYNAK_AD"
	Grid(3,0) = "R_KAYNAKKODU"
	Grid(4,0) = "RECETENOTU"
	Grid(5,0) = "?"
	Grid(6,0) = "R_MIKTART"
	Grid(7,0) = ""
	Grid(8,0) = ""
	Grid(9,0) = "_ISI_DAYANIM"
	
	
	Grid(0,1) = 20
	Grid(1,1) = 20
	Grid(2,1) = 48
	Grid(3,1) = 26
	Grid(4,1) = 26
	Grid(5,1) = 18
	Grid(6,1) = 18
	Grid(7,1) = 15
	Grid(8,1) = 15
	Grid(9,1) = 23
	
	
	Grid(0,2) = "VÝDA TÝPÝ / FEEDER"
	Grid(1,2) = "FORMUL%"
	Grid(2,2) = "HAMMADDE ADI"
	Grid(3,2) = "HAMMADDE KODU"
	Grid(4,2) = "HAMMADDE PARTI NO"
	Grid(5,2) = "KAZAN 1 MIK.(KG)"
	Grid(6,2) = "MIKTAR (KG)"
	Grid(7,2) = "ÝADE"
	Grid(8,2) = "ÝLAVE"
	Grid(9,2) = "HAMMADDE DYN.ISISI °C"
	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "N"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "C"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "N"
	Grid(7,4) = "C"
	Grid(8,4) = "N"
	Grid(9,4) = "N"
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		Degisken = Grid(i,0)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			
			ondalik = MMPS10T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik
		End If
	Next
End Sub

Sub PrintForm()
	Dim pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 280
	UstMargin = 5
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 10 
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 10
	satiryuksekligi = 4.3
	satirBaslangic = 50
	satirsonu=satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*7
	toplamkg=0
	formulkg=0
	std=""
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()


	res = pe1.Open(0, "Satinalma Siparisi",SayfaEni,SayfaBoyu,"LANDSCAPE")

	Call Alanlar()
	If doc.ErrorNumber Then
		MsgBox ("Hata main : " & doc.ErrorNumber)
		Exit Sub
	End If

	satirSayisi = MMPS10T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.SetDevPaperFormat(-2)

	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)

	ReDim Preserve xCizgi(0)
		sol=solmargin

		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 17, Sol, 15, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
			Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)) = Sol - 2
				ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
		Next

			res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 17, Solmargin+230, 15, 40 , "LABORATUVAR AÇIKLAMA")
	res=pe1.setbold(1)
	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",10)
	res=pe1.write(ustmargin+20, solmargin+70, satiryuksekligi, 70, "ÜRÜN REÇETE BÝLGÝSÝ")

	res=pe1.selectfont("arial",9)
	kod=MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(1)
	kodad=MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD(1)
	musteri=MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
	musterad=MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD(1)

Sub PrintForm()

Sub sayfabasi(pe1)

	res = pe1.write(ustmargin+12,solmargin+70,6,100,"ÇEK TEVDÝ BORDROSU (TEMÝNAT / COLLATERAL)")
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.Selectfont("Arial",8)

	res = pe1.write(ustmargin+23,solmargin,6,60,"HESAP SAHÝBÝ / ACCOUNT HOLDER:")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin+23,solmargin+90,6,50,"HESAP NO / ACC NO :")
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin+23,solmargin+150,6,50,"TARÝH / DATE :" & " " & Doc.Strtonordate(tarih))
	res = pe1.write(ustmargin+28,solmargin+150,6,50,"BORDRO NO  :" & " " & (evrakno))

	res = pe1.write(ustmargin+28,solmargin,6,150,"AÞAÐIDA DÖKÜMÜ YAZILI ÇEKLER TESLÝM EDÝLMÝÞTÝR. / WE PRESENT THE CHEQUES LISTED BELOW")

	res= pe1.write(ustmargin+47,solmargin + 56, satiryukseklik,50, "ÞUBESÝ /")
	res= pe1.write(ustmargin+51,solmargin + 56, satiryukseklik,50, "BRANCH")
	res= pe1.write(ustmargin+47,solmargin + 81, satiryukseklik,50, "HESAP NO /")

Sub satiryazdir(pe1, i)

	satirsolmargin=3
	res=pe1.Selectfont("Arial",9)
	sonsatir=pe1.getbottom()
	If sonsatir >= satirsonu Then
	res = pe1.PageBreak()
	sirano=1
	Call sayfabasi(pe1)
	End If
	
	banka=CKSN21T.E_BANKA(i)
	sube=CKSN21T.E_SUBE(i)
	hesapno=CKSN21T.E_HESAPNO(i)
	vade=doc.strtonordate(CKSN21T.E_RVADE(i))
	evrakno=CKSN21T.E_OREVRAKNO(i)
	tutar=CKSN21T.E_OR_TUTAR(i)
	pb=CKSN21T.E_OR_KUR(i)	
	cekno=CKSN21T.E_CKSNNO(i)

	if sirano=1 then
	satirpozr=satirbaslangic
	else
	satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryukseklik
	end if
	satirsolmargin=15
	res= pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res= pe1.write(satirpozr, satirsolmargin-3, satiryukseklik, 10, sirano)
	res= pe1.write(satirpozr, satirsolmargin +8, satiryukseklik, 40, trim(banka))
	res= pe1.write(satirpozr, satirsolmargin +56, satiryukseklik, 40, trim(sube))
	res= pe1.write(satirpozr, satirsolmargin + 81, satiryukseklik, 40, hesapno)
	res= pe1.write(satirpozr, satirsolmargin +106, satiryukseklik, 40, trim(cekno))
	res= pe1.write(satirpozr, satirsolmargin + 131, satiryukseklik, 40, vade)
	res= pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res= pe1.write(satirpozr, satirsolmargin + 145, satiryukseklik, 28, tutar) 
	res= pe1.Setalign("left")
	res= pe1.write(satirpozr, satirsolmargin + 175, satiryukseklik, 28, pb) 

End Sub

Sub sayfasonu(pe1)	
	res= pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	satirp=pe1.getbottom()
	satirp=satirp+10
	yuk=satirsonu-ustmargin-45
	res = pe1.box(ustmargin+45,solmargin,yuk,gen)
	res = pe1.line(ustmargin+45,solmargin+7,yuk,0)
	res = pe1.line(ustmargin+45,solmargin+55,yuk,0)
	res = pe1.line(ustmargin+45,solmargin+80,yuk,0)
	res = pe1.line(ustmargin+45,solmargin+105,yuk,0)
	res = pe1.line(ustmargin+45,solmargin+130,yuk,0)
	res = pe1.line(ustmargin+45,solmargin+155,yuk,0)
	satiryukseklik=4.3
	for i=1 to s
	res = pe1.line(ustmargin+52+i*5,solmargin,0,gen)
	next
	
	res= pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	toplam=Doc.NumFormat(CKSN21E.TL_TOPLAMTUTAR,12,0)
	Yalniz = Doc.NumToText(toplam)

	res= pe1.write(satirsonu+satiryukseklik,satirsolmargin + 95, satiryukseklik,60, "TOPLAM MÝKTAR / TOTAL AMOUNT :")
	res= pe1.Setalign("RIGHT")
	res= pe1.write(satirsonu+satiryukseklik,satirsolmargin + 145, satiryukseklik,28,toplam)

	res= pe1.Setalign("LEFT")
	res= pe1.write(satirsonu+satiryukseklik, satirsolmargin + 175, satiryukseklik, 28, CKSN21T.E_OR_KUR(1)) 

	'res= pe1.write(satirsonu+satiryukseklik*2,solmargin + 8, satiryukseklik,65, "YAZI ÝLE MEBLAÐ / AMOUNT IN WORDS:")
	'res= pe1.write(satirsonu+satiryukseklik*2,solmargin + 82, satiryukseklik,50,Yalniz)

	bbasla5=satirsonu+satiryukseklik*2
	res=pe1.Selectfont("Arial",8)

	res = pe1.setbold(1)	
	res= pe1.write(bbasla5+satiryukseklik,solmargin + 5, satiryukseklik,200, "Yukarýda (veya ekte) dökümü yazýlý çekleri tahsil edildiklerinde bedellerinin Bankanýzca aþaðýda belirtilen kredi lehtarýna")
	res= pe1.write(bbasla5+satiryukseklik*2,solmargin + 5, satiryukseklik,200, "açýlmýþ ve açýlacak krediler ile her ne sebeple olursa olsun doðmuþ doðacak borçlarýna mahsup edilmek üzere")
	res= pe1.write(bbasla5+satiryukseklik*3,solmargin + 5, satiryukseklik,200, "Bankanýza temilk ve teslim ediyorrum/ediyoruz.")
	res= pe1.write(bbasla5+satiryukseklik*4,solmargin + 5, satiryukseklik,200, "I/We assign and deliver to YAPI VE KREDÝ BANKASI the cheques listed hereabove (or attached) for the purpose of")
	res= pe1.write(bbasla5+satiryukseklik*5,solmargin + 5, satiryukseklik,200, "appliying the proceeds upon collection against the credits opened or to be opened and outstandings du for payment or")
	res= pe1.write(bbasla5+satiryukseklik*6,solmargin + 5, satiryukseklik,200, "shall become due for payment of the credit beneficiary mentioned herebelow.")
	res= pe1.write(bbasla5+satiryukseklik*7,solmargin + 5, satiryukseklik,200, "Sözkonusu çeklerin bedellerinin her ne sebeple olursa olsun tahsil edilememesi halinde Bankanýzýn çek sorumlularýnýn")
	res= pe1.write(bbasla5+satiryukseklik*8,solmargin + 5, satiryukseklik,200, "aleyhine kanuni takip yapýp yapmamakta tamamen serbest olduðunu çeklerin zaman aþýmýna uðramasý nedeniyle de")

	res= pe1.write(bbasla5+satiryukseklik*10,solmargin + 5, satiryukseklik,200, "I/we accept tahat if the cheques listed hereabove (or attached) shall not be collected for any reason, YAPI VE KREDÝ")

Public SayfaEni, SayfaBoyu, Yazildi

Sub PrintForm()
	Yazildi = 0
	Set pe1=doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	SayfaEni = 210
	SayfaBoyu = 297
	res=pe1.Open(0,"Mutabakat Mektubu",SayfaBoyu,SayfaEni,"")
	For i = 1 To TL_BORC_ALACAK.GetRecCount()
		If Doc.Is_EY1(TL_BORC_ALACAK.SELECTED(i)) Then
			Call DokumanYarat(pe1,i)
		End If
	Next    
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(0)
End Sub

Sub DokumanYarat(pe1,SatirNo)
	if Yazildi > 0 then res = pe1.PageBreak()

	UstMargin = 25
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = sayfaEni - 10

	CHKodu = TL_BORC_ALACAK.KOD(satirNo)

	CHAdi1 = trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","AD",CHKodu,""))
	CHAdi2 = trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","NAME2",CHKodu,""))
	Adres1 = Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_1",CHKodu,""))
	Adres2 = Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_2",CHKodu,""))
	Adres3 = Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","ADRES_3",CHKodu,""))
	FaxNo  = Trim(Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00","FAXNO",CHKodu,""))
	Firma  = Trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("FNAMEL0"))

	Icerik1 = "Denetçi firmamýz MST YMM VE BAÐIMSIZ DENETÝM LTD.ÞTÝ halen hesaplarýmýzýn olaðan denetimini yapmaktadýr "
	Icerik2 = "Bu denetim ile ilgili olarak 31.12.2009 tarihi ile itibariyle kayýtlarýnýzda görülen hesap bakiyelerine"
	Icerik3 =	"göre aþaðýdaki tablonun doldurularak ekinde cari hesap ekstresi ile en geç iki gün içinde"
	Icerik4 = "aþaðýda adý geçen denetim þirketine ve þirket firma merkezine fakslanmasýný rica eder,"
	Icerik5 = "iþbirliðiniz için teþekkür ederiz"

	Not1 = "MOORE STEPSHENS TÜRKÝYE"
	Not2 = "MST YMM VE BAÐIMSIZ DENETÝM ÞÝRKETÝ"
	Not3 = "Atatürk Mahallesi 42.Parsel Gardenya 5 Plaza Kat:19 Ataþehir"
	Not4 = "Tel : (0 216) 580 92 70 / Fax : (0 216) 580 92 78"
	Not5 = "Firma Merkez (Senkroma) / Tel : (0 212) 875 25 04"

	satirAra = 6

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",12)

	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 1 * satirAra, SolMargin+125, 5, 40,"Tarih : "& Trim(Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun)))
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 2 * satirAra, SolMargin, 5, 80,CHAdi1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 3 * satirAra, SolMargin, 5, 80,CHAdi2)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 4 * satirAra, SolMargin, 5, 80,Adres1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 5 * satirAra, SolMargin, 5, 80,Adres2)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 6 * satirAra, SolMargin, 5, 80,Adres3)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 7 * satirAra, SolMargin, 5, 80,"Faks No :" &" "& FaxNo)

	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin +  9 * satirAra,SolMargin, 10, sagMargin - SolMargin, Icerik1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 11 * satirAra,SolMargin, 5 , sagMargin - SolMargin, Icerik2)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 12 * satirAra,SolMargin, 5 , sagMargin - SolMargin, Icerik3)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 13 * satirAra,SolMargin, 5 , sagMargin - SolMargin, Icerik4)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 14 * satirAra,SolMargin, 5 , sagMargin - SolMargin, Icerik5)

	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 16 * satirAra,SolMargin, 5, sagMargin - SolMargin, Not1)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 17 * satirAra,SolMargin, 5, sagMargin - SolMargin, Not2)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 18 * satirAra,SolMargin, 5, sagMargin - SolMargin, Not3)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 19 * satirAra,SolMargin, 5, sagMargin - SolMargin, Not4)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 20 * satirAra,SolMargin, 5, sagMargin - SolMargin, Not5)


	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 22 * satirAra,SolMargin+120, 5, 30, "Saygýlarýmýzla")

	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 29 * satirAra, SolMargin, 5, sagMargin - SolMargin, "Cari Hesap Adý            Borç Toplamý        Alacak Toplamý       Bakiye     ")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 28 * satirAra, SolMargin, 5, sagMargin - SolMargin, "--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------")

	Yazildi = Yazildi + 1
End Sub

Public 	PHight, PWidth, MLeft, MRight, MTop, MBottom, RHight, RBegin, REnd, BoxWidth, RowHight, SatirPozR

Sub PrintForm()
	PHight = 297
	PWidth = 210
	MLeft = 10
'	MRight= PWidth - 5
	MRight= 10
	MTop  = 10
	MBottom=10 
	RHight = 5
	RBegin = 123
	RCount = 25
	xRowNo = 0
	LastRow= 0
	SatirPozR=0
	xRowNotesLeft = 0
	xRowNotesWidth = ""
	REnd   = MBottom - 25
	BoxWidth = PWidth - (MLeft+MRight)
	RowHight = 5
	xSF_MIKTAR_Left = 0
	xSF_MIKTAR_Width = 0
	xSF_SF_UNIT_Left = 0
	xSF_SF_UNIT_Width = 0
	xOR_FIYAT_Left = 0
	xOR_FIYAT_Width = 0
	xPRICEUNIT_Left = 0
	xPRICEUNIT_Width = 0
	xOR_TUTAR_Left = 0
	xOR_TUTAR_Width = 0

	Set Pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	Res = Pe1.Open(0, "Satinalma Siparisi", PHight, PWidth,"")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",9)
	Call Fields()
	Call PageHeader(pe1)
	For I=1 To STOK46T.GetRecCount()
		Call WriteRows(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call PageFooter(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub PageHeader(pe1)
' -------- 1.Blok Logo, Form No ----------------------------------------------------------------
 	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	Res = Pe1.Box(MTop, MLeft, RowHight*4, BoxWidth)
	xMTop  = MTop+(RowHight*4)+2
	xHight= PHight-(xMTop+MBottom)
	xMLeft = MLeft+(BoxWidth-xWidth)
	xWidth = 55
' ÝKÝNCÝ KUTU
	Res = Pe1.Box(xMTop, MLeft, xHight-20, BoxWidth)
' ÜÇÜNCÜ KUTU
	xxHight = xHight-20
	zHight = xHight - xxHight
	Res = Pe1.Box(xMTop+xxHight, MLeft, zHight, BoxWidth)

	Res = Pe1.line(MTop, MLeft+ 35, RowHight*4, 0)
	Res = Pe1.line(MTop, MLeft+135, RowHight*4, 0)
	Res = Pe1.line(MTop, MLeft+156, RowHight*4, 0)
	xMLeft = MLeft+(BoxWidth-xWidth)
	For J=1 To 3
		Res = Pe1.line(MTop+J*RowHight, xMLeft, 0, xWidth)
	Next
	LogoW = 33
	LogoH = (RowHight*4) - 2
	Logo = Trim(Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO")))
	Res = Pe1.Nopen()
	Res = Pe1.Picture(MTop+1, MLeft+1, LogoH, LogoW, Logo,0)
	Res = Pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	xWidth = 100
	xMLeft = MLeft+36
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
 	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",12)
	Res = Pe1.Write(MTop+  RowHight, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, "SATINALMA FORMU")
	Res = Pe1.Write(MTop+2*RowHight, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, "PURCHASE FORM")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",9)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
 	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	xMLeft = MLeft+136
	xWidth = 20
	Res = Pe1.Write(MTop, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, "Form No")
	Res = Pe1.Write(MTop+RowHight, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, "Yayýn Tarihi")
	Res = Pe1.Write(MTop+2*RowHight, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, "Rev. No")
	Res = Pe1.Write(MTop+3*RowHight, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, "Rev. Tarihi")
	xMLeft = MLeft+165
	Res = Pe1.Write(MTop, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth,"FRM 32")
	Res = Pe1.Write(MTop+RowHight, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, "01/06/2004")
	Res = Pe1.Write(MTop+2*RowHight, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, "0")
' -------- cari hesap / yetkili ----------------------------------------------------------------
	xMTop = MTop + (4*RowHight)+ 2 
	Res = Pe1.line(xMTop, MLeft+ 35, RowHight*4, 0)
	Res = Pe1.line(xMTop, MLeft+135, RowHight*4, 0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",9)
	xWidth = 50
	xMLeft = MLeft+36
	
	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop, MLeft+1, RowHight, xWidth, "SATICI")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",6)
	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop+RowHight-1, MLeft+1, RowHight, xWidth, "VENDOR ")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",9)
	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop+2*RowHight, MLeft+1, RowHight, xWidth, "ÝLGÝLÝ ")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",6)
	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop+3*RowHight-1, MLeft+1, RowHight, xWidth, "TO :")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",9)
	xWidth = 90
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, Trim(STOK46E.CH_KODU_CARI00_AD))
	vChKodu = STOK46E.CH_KODU
	vYetkili= Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00", "KONTAKTNAME_1", vChKodu, "")
	vFAX = Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00", "FAXNO", vChKodu, "")
'	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop+2*RowHight, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, Trim(vYetkili))
	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop+2*RowHight, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, Trim(vYetkili))
	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop+2*RowHight, xMLeft+30, RowHight, xWidth, vFAX)
	xWidth = 50
	xMLeft = MLeft+136
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, "SÝP.EMRÝ NO :" & " " & Trim(STOK46E.EVRAKNO))
	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, "SÝP.EMRÝ NO :" )
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop, xMLeft+23, RowHight, xWidth, Trim(STOK46E.EVRAKNO))

'	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop+2*RowHight, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, "TARÝH  :" & " " & Trim(Doc.StrToNorDate(STOK46E.TARIH)))

' -------- Nakliye Koþullarý / Teslim Süresi ---------------------------------------------------

	xMTop = MTop + (10*RowHight) + 2

	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",8)

	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop+  RowHight-1, (MLeft+xWidth+6), RowHight, xWidth, Trim(STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_1))
	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop+2*RowHight-2, (MLeft+xWidth+6), RowHight, xWidth, Trim(STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_2))
	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop+3*RowHight-3, (MLeft+xWidth+6), RowHight, xWidth, Trim(STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_3))
	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop+4*RowHight-4, (MLeft+xWidth+6), RowHight, xWidth, Trim(STOK46E.SIPLEILGILINOTLAR_4))
' -------- Uyarý Yazý Bloðu --------------------------------------------------------------------
	Res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",7)
	xMTop = MTop + (18*RowHight) + 2 
	Res = Pe1.line(xMTop, MLeft, 0, BoxWidth)

	Res = Pe1.Write(XMTop+1, MLeft+2, RowHight, BoxWidth, "EKTE VERÝLEN TANIMLARA, ÞARTLARA VE TALÝMATLARA UYGUN OLARAK, AÞAÐIDAKÝ ÝSTEDÝÐÝMÝZÝ ÝMAL VE/VEYA TESLÝM EDÝNÝZ")
	Res = Pe1.Write(XMTop+RowHight, MLeft+2, RowHight, BoxWidth, "PLEASE ENTER OUR ORDER FOR THE BELOW MENTIONED SUBJECT TO ALL ATTACHED INSTRUCTIONS, CONDITIONS AND SPECIFICATIONS")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",9)
	xMTop = MTop + (20*RowHight) + 2

 	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	xMTop = MTop + (22*RowHight) + 2

Sub WriteRows(pe1, RowNo)
	If Trim(STOK46T.KOD(RowNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setbold(1)
		Sol = MLeft + 1

			If RowNo = 1 Then
				SatirPozR	= RBegin
			Else
				SatirPozR	= SatirPozR + RHight
			End If

			If xRowNo>RCount Then
				Res = Pe1.PageBreak()
				Call PageHeader(pe1)
				SatirPozR	= RBegin
				xRowNo = 0
			End If

			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK46T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,RowNo)))
				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
					Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,Ondalik)
				ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
					Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
				End If
				If Trim(DegiskenAdi)="_SIRANO" Then
					Degisken = RowNo
				End If

				If Trim(DegiskenAdi)="KOD_STOK00_AD" Then
					xRowNotes = Trim(STOK46T.NOTE0(RowNo))
					xRowNotesLeft = Sol
					xRowNotesWidth= SutunGenisligi
				End If

				If Trim(DegiskenAdi)="SF_MIKTAR" Then
					xSF_MIKTAR_Left = Sol
					xSF_MIKTAR_Width= SutunGenisligi
				End If
				If Trim(DegiskenAdi)="SF_SF_UNIT" Then
					xSF_SF_UNIT_Left = Sol
					xSF_SF_UNIT_Width= SutunGenisligi
				End If
				If Trim(DegiskenAdi)="OR_FIYAT" Then
					xOR_FIYAT_Left = Sol
					xOR_FIYAT_Width= SutunGenisligi
				End If
				If Trim(DegiskenAdi)="PRICEUNIT" Then
					xPRICEUNIT_Left = Sol
					xPRICEUNIT_Width= SutunGenisligi
				End If
				If Trim(DegiskenAdi)="OR_TUTAR" Then
					xOR_TUTAR_Left = Sol
					xOR_TUTAR_Width= SutunGenisligi
				End If

				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, RHight, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			Next					 
			xRowNo = xRowNo + 1
			If Trim(STOK46T.NOTE0(RowNo))<>"" Then
				SatirPozR	= SatirPozR + RHight
				res = pe1.write(SatirPozR, xRowNotesLeft, RHight, xRowNotesWidth-2, xRowNotes)
				xRowNo = xRowNo + 1
			End If

'		xRowNo = xRowNo + 1
		LastRow = Pe1.GetTop()
End Sub

Sub PageFooter(pe1)

	Dim xTable
	xEvrakNo = Trim(STOK46E.EVRAKNO)
'	Set xTable = Doc.Select2G("xStok46", "STOK46T", "EVRAKNO='"+vEvrakNo+"'", "SF_SF_UNIT", "SF_SF_UNIT", -1)
	Set xTable = Doc.Select2G("xStok46", "STOK46T", "", "SF_SF_UNIT", "SF_SF_UNIT", -1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
 	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",8)
	xMTop = LastRow + RowHight
	Res = Pe1.write(xMTop+RowHight, xOR_FIYAT_Left-1, RowHight*2, (xOR_FIYAT_Width+xPRICEUNIT_Width), "TOPLAM/GRAND TOTAL")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
 	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",9)
'	Res = Pe1.write(xMTop+RowHight, xOR_TUTAR_Left, RowHight*2, xOR_TUTAR_Width+3, Doc.NumFormat(STOK46E.OR_KDVDAHILTOPLAM, 20,2)&" " & Trim(STOK46T.PRICEUNIT(1)))
	Res = Pe1.write(xMTop+RowHight, xOR_TUTAR_Left, RowHight*2, xOR_TUTAR_Width+3, Doc.NumFormat(STOK46E.OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM, 20,2)&" " & Trim(STOK46T.PRICEUNIT(1)))

'	OR_ISKONTOLUTOPLAM
 	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",8)
	If xTable.GetRecCount()>0 Then
		For i=1 To xTable.GetRecCount()
			xstmiktar = Doc.NumFormat(xTable.SF_MIKTAR(i), 20, 2)
			xstbirim  = xTable.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
			xLeft= xRowNotesLeft
			xMTop = LastRow + RowHight
		 	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			Res = Pe1.write(xMTop+RowHight*i, xLeft, RowHight, xRowNotesWidth-2, "TOPLAM/TOTAL")
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			Res = Pe1.write(xMTop+RowHight*i, xSF_MIKTAR_Left, RowHight, xSF_MIKTAR_Width-2, xstmiktar)
		 	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
			Res = Pe1.write(xMTop+RowHight*i, xSF_SF_UNIT_Left, RowHight,xSF_SF_UNIT_Width-2,Trim(xstbirim))
			xRowNo = xRowNo + 1
		Next
	End If

	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",8)

End Sub

Sub Fields()
	ReDim Grid(8, 8)
	Grid(0, 0) = "_SIRANO"
	Grid(1, 0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2, 0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(3, 0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(4, 0) = "OR_FIYAT"
	Grid(5, 0) = "PRICEUNIT"
	Grid(6, 0) = "OR_TUTAR"
	Grid(7, 0) = "PRICEUNIT"

	Grid(0, 1) = 10
	Grid(1, 1) = 74
	Grid(2, 1) = 20
	Grid(3, 1) = 15
	Grid(4, 1) = 20
	Grid(5, 1) = 13
	Grid(6, 1) = 25
	Grid(7, 1) = 13

	Grid(0, 2) = "SIRA"
	Grid(1, 2) = "MAL VE/VEYA HÝZMETÝN CÝNS VE NÝTELÝÐÝ"
	Grid(2, 2) = "MÝKTAR"
	Grid(3, 2) = "BÝRÝM"
	Grid(4, 2) = "BÝRÝM FIYAT"
	Grid(5, 2) = ""
	Grid(6, 2) = "TUTAR"
	Grid(7, 2) = ""

	Grid(0, 3) = "ITEM"
	Grid(1, 3) = "DESCRIPTION"
	Grid(2, 3) = "QUANTITY"
	Grid(3, 3) = "UNIT"
	Grid(4, 3) = "UNIT PRICE"
	Grid(5, 3) = ""
	Grid(6, 3) = "TOTAL PRICE"
	Grid(7, 3) = ""

	Grid(0, 4) = "N"
	Grid(1, 4) = "C"
	Grid(2, 4) = "N"
	Grid(3, 4) = "C"
	Grid(4, 4) = "N"
	Grid(5, 4) = "C"
	Grid(6, 4) = "N"
	Grid(7, 4) = "C"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid, 1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i, 0)
		If Grid(i, 4) = "N" Then
			ondalik = STOK46T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i, 6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
End Sub

Public 	PHight, PWidth, MLeft, MRight, MTop, MBottom, RHight, RBegin, REnd, BoxWidth, RowHight, SatirPozR
Public xSF_MIKTAR_Left, xSF_MIKTAR_Width, xSF_SF_UNIT_Left, xSF_SF_UNIT_Width, xOR_FIYAT_Left, xOR_FIYAT_Width, xPRICEUNIT_Left, xPRICEUNIT_Width, xOR_TUTAR_Left, xOR_TUTAR_Width
Public RCount, xRowNo, LastRow, xRowNotesLeft, xRowNotesWidth
Dim Grid
Sub PrintForm()
	PHight = 297
	PWidth = 210
	MLeft = 10
'	MRight= PWidth - 5
	MRight= 10
	MTop  = 10
	MBottom=10 
	RHight = 5
	RBegin = 123
	RCount = 25
	xRowNo = 0
	LastRow= 0
	SatirPozR=0
	xRowNotesLeft = 0
	xRowNotesWidth = ""
	REnd   = MBottom - 25
	BoxWidth = PWidth - (MLeft+MRight)
	RowHight = 5
	xSF_MIKTAR_Left = 0
	xSF_MIKTAR_Width = 0
	xSF_SF_UNIT_Left = 0
	xSF_SF_UNIT_Width = 0
	xOR_FIYAT_Left = 0
	xOR_FIYAT_Width = 0
	xPRICEUNIT_Left = 0
	xPRICEUNIT_Width = 0
	xOR_TUTAR_Left = 0
	xOR_TUTAR_Width = 0

	Set Pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	Res = Pe1.Open(0, "Satinalma Siparisi", PHight, PWidth,"")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",9)
	Call Fields()
	Call PageHeader(pe1)
	For I=1 To STOK46T.GetRecCount()
		Call WriteRows(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call PageFooter(pe1)
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub PageHeader(pe1)
' -------- 1.Blok Logo, Form No ----------------------------------------------------------------
 	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	Res = Pe1.Box(MTop, MLeft, RowHight*4, BoxWidth)
	xMTop  = MTop+(RowHight*4)+2
	xHight= PHight-(xMTop+MBottom)
	xMLeft = MLeft+(BoxWidth-xWidth)
	xWidth = 55
' ÝKÝNCÝ KUTU
	Res = Pe1.Box(xMTop, MLeft, xHight-20, BoxWidth)
' ÜÇÜNCÜ KUTU
	xxHight = xHight-20
	zHight = xHight - xxHight
	Res = Pe1.Box(xMTop+xxHight, MLeft, zHight, BoxWidth)

	Res = Pe1.line(MTop, MLeft+ 35, RowHight*4, 0)
	Res = Pe1.line(MTop, MLeft+135, RowHight*4, 0)
	Res = Pe1.line(MTop, MLeft+156, RowHight*4, 0)
	xMLeft = MLeft+(BoxWidth-xWidth)
	For J=1 To 3
		Res = Pe1.line(MTop+J*RowHight, xMLeft, 0, xWidth)
	Next
	LogoW = 33
	LogoH = (RowHight*4) - 2
	Logo = Trim(Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm() & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO")))
	Res = Pe1.Nopen()
	Res = Pe1.Picture(MTop+1, MLeft+1, LogoH, LogoW, Logo,0)
	Res = Pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	xWidth = 100
	xMLeft = MLeft+36
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
 	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",12)
	Res = Pe1.Write(MTop+  RowHight, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, "SATINALMA FORMU")
	Res = Pe1.Write(MTop+2*RowHight, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, "PURCHASE FORM")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",9)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
 	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	xMLeft = MLeft+136
	xWidth = 20
	Res = Pe1.Write(MTop, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, "Form No")
	Res = Pe1.Write(MTop+RowHight, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, "Yayýn Tarihi")
	Res = Pe1.Write(MTop+2*RowHight, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, "Rev. No")
	Res = Pe1.Write(MTop+3*RowHight, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, "Rev. Tarihi")

' -------- cari hesap / yetkili ----------------------------------------------------------------
	xMTop = MTop + (4*RowHight)+ 2 
	Res = Pe1.line(xMTop, MLeft+ 35, RowHight*4, 0)
	Res = Pe1.line(xMTop, MLeft+135, RowHight*4, 0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",9)
	xWidth = 50
	xMLeft = MLeft+36
	
	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop, MLeft+1, RowHight, xWidth, "SATICI")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",6)
	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop+RowHight-1, MLeft+1, RowHight, xWidth, "VENDOR ")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",9)
	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop+2*RowHight, MLeft+1, RowHight, xWidth, "ÝLGÝLÝ ")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",6)
	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop+3*RowHight-1, MLeft+1, RowHight, xWidth, "TO :")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",9)
	xWidth = 90
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, Trim(STOK46E.CH_KODU_CARI00_AD))
	vChKodu = STOK46E.CH_KODU
	vYetkili= Doc.GetDbFieldValue("CARI00", "KONTAKTNAME_1", vChKodu, "")

'	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop+2*RowHight, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, Trim(vYetkili))
	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop+2*RowHight, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, Trim(vYetkili))

'	Res = Pe1.Write(xMTop, xMLeft, RowHight, xWidth, "SÝP.EMRÝ NO :" & " " & Trim(STOK46E.EVRAKNO))

Sub WriteRows(pe1, RowNo)
	If Trim(STOK46T.KOD(RowNo)) = "" Then Exit Sub
		Degisken = ""
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 9)
		res = pe1.setbold(1)
		Sol = MLeft + 1

			If RowNo = 1 Then
				SatirPozR	= RBegin
			Else
				SatirPozR	= SatirPozR + RHight
			End If

			If xRowNo>RCount Then
				Res = Pe1.PageBreak()
				Call PageHeader(pe1)
				SatirPozR	= RBegin
				xRowNo = 0
			End If

			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) 
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
				Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK46T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,RowNo)))
				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
					Degisken = Doc.NumFormat(Degisken, 20,Ondalik)
				ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
					Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
				End If
				If Trim(DegiskenAdi)="_SIRANO" Then
					Degisken = RowNo
				End If

				If Trim(DegiskenAdi)="KOD_STOK00_AD" Then
					xRowNotes = Trim(STOK46T.NOTE0(RowNo))
					xRowNotesLeft = Sol
					xRowNotesWidth= SutunGenisligi
				End If

				If Trim(DegiskenAdi)="SF_MIKTAR" Then
					xSF_MIKTAR_Left = Sol
					xSF_MIKTAR_Width= SutunGenisligi
				End If
				If Trim(DegiskenAdi)="SF_SF_UNIT" Then
					xSF_SF_UNIT_Left = Sol
					xSF_SF_UNIT_Width= SutunGenisligi
				End If
				If Trim(DegiskenAdi)="OR_FIYAT" Then
					xOR_FIYAT_Left = Sol
					xOR_FIYAT_Width= SutunGenisligi
				End If
				If Trim(DegiskenAdi)="PRICEUNIT" Then
					xPRICEUNIT_Left = Sol
					xPRICEUNIT_Width= SutunGenisligi
				End If
				If Trim(DegiskenAdi)="OR_TUTAR" Then
					xOR_TUTAR_Left = Sol
					xOR_TUTAR_Width= SutunGenisligi
				End If

				res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, RHight, SutunGenisligi - 2, Degisken)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			Next					 
			xRowNo = xRowNo + 1
			If Trim(STOK46T.NOTE0(RowNo))<>"" Then
				SatirPozR	= SatirPozR + RHight
				res = pe1.write(SatirPozR, xRowNotesLeft, RHight, xRowNotesWidth-2, xRowNotes)
				xRowNo = xRowNo + 1
			End If

'		xRowNo = xRowNo + 1
		LastRow = Pe1.GetTop()
End Sub

Sub PageFooter(pe1)

	Dim xTable
	xEvrakNo = Trim(STOK46E.EVRAKNO)
'	Set xTable = Doc.Select2G("xStok46", "STOK46T", "EVRAKNO='"+vEvrakNo+"'", "SF_SF_UNIT", "SF_SF_UNIT", -1)
	Set xTable = Doc.Select2G("xStok46", "STOK46T", "", "SF_SF_UNIT", "SF_SF_UNIT", -1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
 	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",8)
	xMTop = LastRow + RowHight
	Res = Pe1.write(xMTop+RowHight, xOR_FIYAT_Left-1, RowHight*2, (xOR_FIYAT_Width+xPRICEUNIT_Width), "TOPLAM/GRAND TOTAL")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
 	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",9)
	Res = Pe1.write(xMTop+RowHight, xOR_TUTAR_Left, RowHight*2, xOR_TUTAR_Width+3, Doc.NumFormat(STOK46E.OR_KDVDAHILTOPLAM, 20,2)&" " & Trim(STOK46T.PRICEUNIT(1)))
 	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",8)
	If xTable.GetRecCount()>0 Then
		For i=1 To xTable.GetRecCount()
			xstmiktar = Doc.NumFormat(xTable.SF_MIKTAR(i), 20, 2)
			xstbirim  = xTable.SF_SF_UNIT(i)
			xLeft= xRowNotesLeft
			xMTop = LastRow + RowHight
		 	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
			Res = Pe1.write(xMTop+RowHight*i, xLeft, RowHight, xRowNotesWidth-2, "TOPLAM/TOTAL")
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			Res = Pe1.write(xMTop+RowHight*i, xSF_MIKTAR_Left, RowHight, xSF_MIKTAR_Width-2, xstmiktar)
		 	Res = Pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
			Res = Pe1.write(xMTop+RowHight*i, xSF_SF_UNIT_Left, RowHight,xSF_SF_UNIT_Width-2,Trim(xstbirim))
			xRowNo = xRowNo + 1
		Next
	End If

	res = pe1.SelectFont("ARIAL",8)

End Sub

Sub Fields()
	ReDim Grid(8, 8)
	Grid(0, 0) = "_SIRANO"
	Grid(1, 0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2, 0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(3, 0) = "SF_SF_UNIT"
	Grid(4, 0) = "OR_FIYAT"
	Grid(5, 0) = "PRICEUNIT"
	Grid(6, 0) = "OR_TUTAR"
	Grid(7, 0) = "PRICEUNIT"

	Grid(0, 1) = 10
	Grid(1, 1) = 74
	Grid(2, 1) = 20
	Grid(3, 1) = 15
	Grid(4, 1) = 20
	Grid(5, 1) = 13
	Grid(6, 1) = 25
	Grid(7, 1) = 13

	Grid(0, 2) = "SIRA"
	Grid(1, 2) = "MAL VE/VEYA HÝZMETÝN CÝNS VE NÝTELÝÐÝ"
	Grid(2, 2) = "MÝKTAR"
	Grid(3, 2) = "BÝRÝM"
	Grid(4, 2) = "BÝRÝM FIYAT"
	Grid(5, 2) = ""
	Grid(6, 2) = "TUTAR"
	Grid(7, 2) = ""

	Grid(0, 3) = "ITEM"
	Grid(1, 3) = "DESCRIPTION"
	Grid(2, 3) = "QUANTITY"
	Grid(3, 3) = "UNIT"
	Grid(4, 3) = "UNIT PRICE"
	Grid(5, 3) = ""
	Grid(6, 3) = "TOTAL PRICE"
	Grid(7, 3) = ""

	Grid(0, 4) = "N"
	Grid(1, 4) = "C"
	Grid(2, 4) = "N"
	Grid(3, 4) = "C"
	Grid(4, 4) = "N"
	Grid(5, 4) = "C"
	Grid(6, 4) = "N"
	Grid(7, 4) = "C"

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid, 1) 
		Degisken = Grid(i, 0)
		If Grid(i, 4) = "N" Then
			ondalik = STOK46T.MaxSigDig(Degisken)	
			Grid(i, 6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
End Sub

Sub PrintForm()
	QStr = "STOK_CODE='" & MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU & "'&RECTYPE<>'D'&RECTYPE<>'P'&SF_MIKTAR>0&EVRAKNO<>'" & MMPS10E.EVRAKNO & "'"
	Set S32E_Old = Doc.Select1("fsefs4f4fsf", "SFDC32E", QStr, "<RECTARIH1",1)
	If S32E_Old.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		QStr = "EVRAKNO='" & S32E_Old.MPSNO(1) & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'&R_KAYNAKKODU<>'" & MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU & "'"
		Set M10T_Old = Doc.Select1("sef43ffefeg", "MMPS10T", QStr, "", -1)
	End If
	Dim pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	UstMargin = 5
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 10
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 15
	satiryuksekligi = 4.3
	satirBaslangic = 50
	satirsonu = satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*7
	toplamkg = 0
	formulkg = 0
	std = ""
	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()	
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Uretim Takip Formu",SayfaEni,SayfaBoyu,"LANDSCAPE")
	
	Call Alanlar()
	
	satirSayisi = MMPS10T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	if S32E_Old.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		if M10T_Old.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			For i = 1 to M10T_Old.GetRowCount()
				Call EskiMPSSatirYazdir(pe1, i)
			Next
		End if
	End if
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
'	res = pe1.SetDevPaperFormat(-2)
	
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	baslangic = ustmargin+3
	satirAra = 4
	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	ReDim Preserve xCizgi(0)
	sol = solmargin - 1
	
	eskiSol = 0
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If eskiSol = 0 and Left(Grid(i,0), 5) = "_ESKI" Then eskiSol = sol
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		res = pe1.Write(satirBaslangic - 17, Sol, 15, SutunGenisligi, Baslik)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)) = Sol - 1
		ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
	Next

	If S32E_Old.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		OncekiMakina = S32E_Old.X_T_ISMERKEZI
		AlinanHmd = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_Old.EVRAKNO,"INPUT",0)
		IadeEdilenHmd = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_Old.EVRAKNO,"IADE",0)
		KullanilanHammadde = AlinanHmd - IadeEdilenHmd

		MakinaBitisSaati = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_Old.EVRAKNO,"ME",0)
		MakinaBaslangicSaati = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_Old.EVRAKNO,"MB",0)
		UretimSuresi = MakinaBitisSaati - MakinaBaslangicSaati

		UretimHizi = Doc.Div(KullanilanHammadde, UretimSuresi)
		UretimSonucu = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E", S32E_Old.EVRAKNO, "LBC", 0)
		OncekiMPSNo = S32E_Old.MPSNO
		OncekiUretimTarihi = S32E_Old.RECTARIH1
	End if
	res = pe1.Write(Baslangic, eskiSol, 5, sagMargin - eskiSol - 1, "Önceki Üretim Bilgileri")
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Gen40 = 30
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, eskiSol, 5, Gen40, "Kullanýlan Makina")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & trim(OncekiMakina))	

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, eskiSol, 5, Gen40, "Üretim Hýzý (kg/saat)")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & Doc.NumFormat(UretimHizi, 12, 2))	

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, eskiSol, 5, Gen40, "Üretim Sonucu (A/B)")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & UretimSonucu)	

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, eskiSol, 5, Gen40, "Önceki MPS No")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & trim(OncekiMPSNo))	

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, eskiSol, 5, Gen40, "Önceki Üretim Tarihi")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(OncekiUretimTarihi))	


	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.Write(ustmargin+22, solmargin+90, satiryuksekligi, 70, "ÜRÜN REÇETE BÝLGÝSÝ")
	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	kod = MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(1)
	kodad = MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD(1)
	musteri = MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
	musterad = MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD(1)
	sipno = MMPS10E.MUSTERISIPINO(1)
	sipartno = MMPS10E.MUSTERISIPIartNO(1)
	sipmiktar = Doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK40T", "SF_MIKTAR", sipartno, "ARTNO")
	testarih = MMPS10E.URETIMDENTESTARIH(1)
	mpsno = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO(1)
	miktar = MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(1)
	not1 = MMPS10E.NOTLAR_1(1)
	not2 = MMPS10E.NOTLAR_2(1)
	parti = MMPS10E.NOTLAR_1
	
	rows = Doc.SelectEQ("BOMU01E", "MAMULCODE", kod)
	Set Old = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_BOMU01E_SELECT")
	For i = 1 to rows
		
		aktif = Old.AKTIF_PASIF(i)
		If aktif = "*" then
			kons = Old.ACIKLAMA(i)
			evno = old.EVRAKNO(i)
			std = Doc.GetUDFValue("BOMU01E",evno,"STD",0)
			
		End If
	Next
		
	Gen10 = 25
	Gen11 = 65
	Gen20 = 22
	Gen21 = 26
	Gen30 = 25
	Gen31 = 45

	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Lucida Console", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(baslangic, solmargin, 6, Gen10, "Ürün Adý")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11, ": " & trim(kod) & " " & Trim(kodad))
	Blok20 = pe1.GetRight + 1

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solmargin, 6, Gen10, "Parti No")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11,": " & trim(parti))

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solmargin, 6, Gen10, "Müþteri")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11, ": " & trim(musterad))	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solmargin, 6, Gen10, "Üretilecek Miktar")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11, ": " & Doc.numformat_nlz(miktar,12,2))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solmargin, 6, Gen10, "Makina Adý")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11, ": ")

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solmargin, 6, Gen10, "Standart No")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11, ": " & trim(std))

	res = pe1.Write(baslangic, Blok20, satiryuksekligi, Gen20, "Konsantrasyon")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen21, ": " & trim(kons))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok20, 6, Gen20, "Sipariþ No")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen21, ": " & trim(sipno))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok20, 6, Gen20, "Sipariþ Miktarý")	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen21, ": " & Doc.numformat_nlz(sipmiktar,12,2))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok20, 6, Gen20,"Teslim Tarihi")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen21,": " & Doc.strtonordate(testarih))
	Blok30 = pe1.GetRight + 1
	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(baslangic, Blok30, 6, Gen30,"MPS No")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen31,": " & trim(mpsno))
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok30, 6, Gen30, "Ürt. Baþ. Saati")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen31,":" )
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok30, 6, Gen30,"Ürt.Baþ./Bit.Saati")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen31,":" )
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok30, 6, Gen30, "Ürt.Bit.Saati")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen31,":" )
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok30, 6, Gen30, "Form No ")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen31,": 39")
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)	
	Dim satirpozr
	sol = solmargin - 1
	If trim(MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(satirNo)) = "I" Then Exit Sub

	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic-8.6
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic-8.6
		Else
			satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
		End If
	End If
	hammaddekod = MMPS10T.TRNUM(satirno)
	hammaddekod = MMPS10T.SRNUM(satirno)
	hammaddekod1 = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirno)
	Set S00 = Doc.Select1("dwedwdasfg", "STOK00", "KOD='" & MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo) & "'", "", -1)

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		AlanDegeri = ""
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
		If AlanAdi = "?" then
			AlanDegeri = ""
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "_FORMUL" then
			if MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo) <> MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU or Basilacak Then
				AlanDegeri = Doc.Div(MMPS10T.R_MIKTART(satirNo), MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR) * 100
				formulkg = formulkg + AlanDegeri
				res = pe1.SelectFont("Lucida Console", 12)
			End if
			solFormul = sol
			GenFormul = sutunGenisligi			
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "DM" then
			kaynakkodu = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirno)
			DM = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10T",trim(jobno),"DM",0)
			AlanDegeri = trim(DM)
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "VIDA" then
			kaynakkodu = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirno)
			vida = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10T",trim(jobno),"VIDA",0)
			AlanDegeri = trim(vida)
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "_ISI_DAYANIM" Then
			AlanDegeri = S00.B_B_TECH_5
		Else
			AlanDegeri = MMPS10T.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,SatirNo)
		End If
		If AlanAdi = "KAYNAK_AD" then
			AlanDegeri = S00.AD
			if Len(trim(AlanDegeri)) > 30 Then
				res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 9)
			End if
		End If
		
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			If AlanAdi = "!" then
				ondalik = 2
			else
				Ondalik = 3
			End If
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			AlanDegeri = Doc.numformat_NLZ(AlanDegeri, 18, ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.StrtoNordate(AlanDegeri)
		End If
		If AlanAdi = "R_MIKTART" then
			toplamkg = toplamkg+AlanDegeri
			solToplam = sol
			GenToplam = sutunGenisligi
			res = pe1.SelectFont("Lucida Console", 10)
		End If
		
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Sol, 6, SutunGenisligi - 2, AlanDegeri)
		
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next
End Sub
Sub EskiMPSSatirYazdir(pe1, satirNo)
	If satirNo = 1 Then
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic-8.6
	Else
		satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
	End If
	sol = solMargin - 1
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 8)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		If AlanAdi = "_ESKI_HMD" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", M10T_Old.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo), "")
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "_ESKI_FORMUL" Then
			MPSMiktari = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", M10T_Old.EVRAKNO(satirNo), "")
			AlanDegeri = Doc.Div(M10T_Old.R_MIKTART(satirNo), MPSMiktari) * 100
			res = pe1.SelectFont("Lucida Console", 8)
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "_ESKI_VIDA" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10T", M10T_Old.JOBNO(satirNo), "VIDA", 0)
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "_ESKI_DOZAJ" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10T", M10T_Old.JOBNO(satirNo), "DM", 0)
		Else
			AlanDegeri = ""
		End If
		
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = 3
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			AlanDegeri = Doc.numformat_NLZ(AlanDegeri, 18, ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.StrtoNordate(AlanDegeri)
		End If
		if trim(CStr(AlanDegeri)) <> "" Then
			res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Sol, 6, SutunGenisligi - 2, AlanDegeri)
		End if
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*6+1, solmargin, satiryuksekligi, 20, "TOPLAM :")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*6+1, solFormul, satiryuksekligi, GenFormul - 2,Doc.numformat(formulkg,12,2))
	res = pe1.Write(satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*6+1, solToplam, satiryuksekligi, GenToplam - 2,Doc.numformat(toplamkg,12,3))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	
	
	
	son2 = satirsonu+satiryuksekligi*4
	
	'DÝKEY ÇÝZGÝLER (SUTUN ÇÝZGÝLERÝ)
	For i = 0 To UBound(grid,1) - 1
		LineX = cint(xCizgi(i))
		If LineX = 0 Then Exit For
		res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 18, LineX, 44, 0)
	Next
	
	'-------satýrlar için cerceve ve cizgiler
	res = pe1.Box(satirBaslangic - 18, solmargin-2, 44, sagMargin - solmargin+2)
	res = pe1.Box(UstMargin + 3, eskiSol - 1, satirBaslangic - UstMargin - 21, sagMargin - EskiSol + 1)
	
	for i = 0 to 7
		res = pe1.Line((satirBaslangic-9)+i*satiryuksekligi, solmargin-2, 0, sagMargin - solmargin+2)
	next
	
	'res = pe1.Box(satirBaslangic - 18, solmargin+228, 44, 33)
	'res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic-9,solmargin+228,0,33)
	
	
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	'---------SICAKLIKLAR
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	satirsonu1 = satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*8
	
	res = pe1.Box(satirsonu1, solmargin-2, 21, 210)
	KutuSag1 = pe1.GetRight()
	res = pe1.Line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin-2, 0, pe1.GetRight - pe1.GetLeft)
	res = pe1.Box(satirsonu1, solmargin+213, 50, 48)
	res = pe1.Line(satirsonu1+9, solmargin+213, 0, 48)
	res = pe1.Line(satirsonu1+40, solmargin+213, 0, 48)
	Gen11 = 10
	For i = 1 to 9
		res = pe1.Line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin + 10 + (i-1) * Gen11, 16, 0)
	Next
	res = pe1.Line(satirsonu1, pe1.GetLeft + Gen11, 21, 0)
	SicaklikSag = pe1.GetRight

	res = pe1.Line(satirsonu1+13, solmargin-2, 0, pe1.GetRight - solMargin + 2)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu1+1, solmargin+30, satiryuksekligi, 70, "SICAKLIK BÝLGÝLERÝ")

	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu1+3, solmargin+222, satiryuksekligi, 40, "ÜRETÝM AÇIKLAMA")
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu1+12, solmargin+214, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Premix Baþ.Saati")
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu1+17, solmargin+214, satiryuksekligi, 40, "Premix Bit.Saati")
	
	kod = MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(1)
	rows = Doc.SelectEQ("BOMU01E", "MAMULCODE", kod)
	Set Old = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_BOMU01E_SELECT")
	For i = 1 to rows	
		aktif = Old.AKTIF_PASIF(i)
		If aktif = "*" then
			kons = Old.ACIKLAMA(i)
			evno = old.EVRAKNO(i)
			std = Doc.GetUDFValue("BOMU01E",evno,"STD",0)
		End If
	Next
	
	If trim(std) = "" then
		res = pe1.Write(satirsonu1+43, solmargin+215, satiryuksekligi, 45,"Numune Miktarý : 0.5 kg")
	else
		res = pe1.Write(satirsonu1+43, solmargin+215, satiryuksekligi, 45,"Numune Miktarý : 0.15 kg")
	End If

	if S32E_Old.GetRowCount() > 0 Then	
		oner1 = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E", S32E_Old.EVRAKNO, "ON1", 0)
		oner2 = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E", S32E_Old.EVRAKNO, "ON2", 0)
		oner3 = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E", S32E_Old.EVRAKNO, "ON3", 0)
		oner4 = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E", S32E_Old.EVRAKNO, "ON4", 0)
		oner5 = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E", S32E_Old.EVRAKNO, "ON5", 0)
		oner6 = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E", S32E_Old.EVRAKNO, "ON6", 0)
		oner7 = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E", S32E_Old.EVRAKNO, "ON7", 0)
		oner8 = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E", S32E_Old.EVRAKNO, "ON8", 0)
		oner9 = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E", S32E_Old.EVRAKNO, "ON9", 0)
	End if

	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu1 + 7 , solMargin, satiryuksekligi, 10, "ÖNR")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 9, satiryuksekligi, Gen11, oner1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, Gen11,oner2)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, Gen11,oner3)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, Gen11,oner4)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, Gen11,oner5)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, Gen11,oner6)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, Gen11,oner7)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, Gen11,oner8)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, Gen11,oner9)
		
	B = solmargin+5
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 8, solMargin, satiryuksekligi, 10, "GRÇ")	

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu1+1, SicaklikSag, satiryuksekligi, KutuSag1 - SicaklikSag, "LABORATUVAR AÇIKLAMA")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	GidaUygunluk = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "BI_FOOD_AP", 0)
	If trim(GidaUygunluk) <> "" Then
		res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 16)
		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 6, SicaklikSag + 1, 20, KutuSag1 - SicaklikSag - 2 , "GIDAYA UYGUNLUK : " & trim(GidaUygunluk))
		res = pe1.UseSet(1)
	End If
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)


	satirsonu2 = satirsonu1+25
	res = pe1.Box(satirsonu2, solmargin-2, 25, 210)
	res = pe1.Line(satirsonu2+5, solmargin-2, 0, 210)
	res = pe1.Line(satirsonu2+10, solmargin-2, 0, 210)
	res = pe1.Line(satirsonu2+15, solmargin-2, 0, 210)

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(13,7)
	
	Grid(0,0) = "DM"
	Grid(1,0) = "VIDA"
	Grid(2,0) = "_FORMUL"
	Grid(3,0) = "KAYNAK_AD"
	Grid(4,0) = "R_KAYNAKKODU"
	Grid(5,0) = "RECETENOTU"
	Grid(6,0) = "R_MIKTART"
	Grid(7,0) = ""
	Grid(8,0) = ""
	Grid(9,0) = "_ISI_DAYANIM"
	Grid(10,0) = "_ESKI_HMD"
	Grid(11,0) = "_ESKI_FORMUL"
	Grid(12,0) = "_ESKI_VIDA"
	Grid(13,0) = "_ESKI_DOZAJ"
	
	Grid(0,1) = 10
	Grid(1,1) = 13
	Grid(2,1) = 18
	Grid(3,1) = 61
	Grid(4,1) = 26
	Grid(5,1) = 22
	Grid(6,1) = 18
	Grid(7,1) = 15
	Grid(8,1) = 15
	Grid(9,1) = 14
	Grid(10,1) = 28
	Grid(11,1) = 12
	Grid(12,1) = 11
	Grid(13,1) = 10
	
	Grid(0,2) = "DZJ. MT."
	Grid(1,2) = "VÝDA TÝPÝ"
	Grid(2,2) = "FORMÜL"
	Grid(3,2) = "HAMMADDE ADI"
	Grid(4,2) = "HAMMADDE KODU"
	Grid(5,2) = "HAMMADDE PARTÝ NO"
	Grid(6,2) = "MÝKTAR"
	Grid(7,2) = "ÝADE"
	Grid(8,2) = "ÝLAVE"
	Grid(9,2) = "HMD.DY. IS.°C"
	Grid(10,2) = "Hammade Adý"
	Grid(11,2) = "Formül" & vbcrlf & "%"
	Grid(12,2) = "Vida Tipi"
	Grid(13,2) = "Dzj. Mt."
	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "N"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "C"
	Grid(5,4) = "C"
	Grid(6,4) = "N"
	Grid(7,4) = "C"
	Grid(8,4) = "N"
	Grid(9,4) = "N"
	Grid(10,4) = "C"
	Grid(11,4) = "N"
	Grid(12,4) = "C"
	Grid(13,4) = "C"
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
			Grid(i,6) = MMPS10T.MaxSigDig(AlanAdi)
		End If
	Next
End Sub
Function Basilacak()
	Basilacak = False
	Set M10TH1 = Doc.Select2G("fw4fs", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'", "", "EVRAKNO", -1)
	if M10TH1.GetRowCount = 0 Then Exit Function
	
	if Doc.Round(M10TH1.R_MIKTART(1), 3) = Doc.Round(MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, 3) Then Basilacak = True
End Function
