Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SENK_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : MPS12-2
Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için : 
Açýklama : ÜRETÝM TAKÝP FORMU / SENKROMA / yeni (baskýlý kaðýt için)
Kod : 
Public sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu, kutuSag, eskiSol, S32E_Old, M10T_Old
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic, SatirBitis
Public AlanSayisi(), ToplamTutar, ToplamProTutar, Kod, ondalik1, toplamkg, formulkg, Std
Public xCizgi(), Grid(), solFormul, GenFormul, solToplam, GenToplam

Sub PrintForm()
	QStr = "STOK_CODE='" & MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU & "'&RECTYPE<>'D'&RECTYPE<>'P'&SF_MIKTAR>0&MPSNO<>'" & MMPS10E.EVRAKNO & "'"
	Set S32E_Old = Doc.Select1("fsefs4f4fsf", "SFDC32E", QStr, "<RECTARIH1",1)
	If S32E_Old.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		QStr = "EVRAKNO='" & S32E_Old.MPSNO(1) & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'&R_KAYNAKKODU<>'" & MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU & "'"
		Set M10T_Old = Doc.Select1("sef43ffefeg", "MMPS10T", QStr, "", -1)
	End If
	Dim pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	UstMargin = 5
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 10
	SolMargin = 12
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 15
	satiryuksekligi = 4.3
	satirBaslangic = 50
	satirsonu = satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*6
	toplamkg = 0
	formulkg = 0
	std = ""
	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()	
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Uretim Takip Formu",SayfaEni,SayfaBoyu,"LANDSCAPE")
	
	Call Alanlar()
	
	satirSayisi = MMPS10T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	if S32E_Old.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		if M10T_Old.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			For i = 1 to M10T_Old.GetRowCount()
				Call EskiMPSSatirYazdir(pe1, i)
			Next
		End if
	End if
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
'	res = pe1.SetDevPaperFormat(-2)
	
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	baslangic = ustmargin+3
	satirAra = 4
	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	ReDim Preserve xCizgi(0)
	sol = solmargin - 1
	
	eskiSol = 0
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If eskiSol = 0 and Left(Grid(i,0), 5) = "_ESKI" Then eskiSol = sol
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		res = pe1.Write(satirBaslangic - 17, Sol, 15, SutunGenisligi, Baslik)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)) = Sol - 1
		ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
	Next

	If S32E_Old.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		OncekiMakina = S32E_Old.X_T_ISMERKEZI
		AlinanHmd = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_Old.EVRAKNO,"INPUT",0)
		IadeEdilenHmd = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_Old.EVRAKNO,"IADE",0)
		KullanilanHammadde = AlinanHmd - IadeEdilenHmd

		MakinaBitisSaati = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_Old.EVRAKNO,"ME",0)
		MakinaBaslangicSaati = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_Old.EVRAKNO,"MB",0)
		UretimSuresi = MakinaBitisSaati - MakinaBaslangicSaati

		UretimHizi = Doc.Div(KullanilanHammadde, UretimSuresi)
		UretimSonucu = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E", S32E_Old.EVRAKNO, "LBC", 0)
		OncekiMPSNo = S32E_Old.MPSNO
		OncekiUretimTarihi = S32E_Old.RECTARIH1
	End if
	res = pe1.Write(Baslangic, eskiSol, 5, sagMargin - eskiSol - 1, "Önceki Üretim Bilgileri")
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Gen40 = 30
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, eskiSol, 5, Gen40, "Kullanýlan Makina")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & trim(OncekiMakina))	

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, eskiSol, 5, Gen40, "Üretim Hýzý (kg/saat)")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & Doc.NumFormat(UretimHizi, 12, 2))	

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, eskiSol, 5, Gen40, "Üretim Sonucu (A/B)")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & UretimSonucu)	

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, eskiSol, 5, Gen40, "Önceki MPS No")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & trim(OncekiMPSNo))	

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, eskiSol, 5, Gen40, "Önceki Üretim Tarihi")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(OncekiUretimTarihi))	


	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.Write(ustmargin+22, solmargin+90, satiryuksekligi, 70, "ÜRÜN REÇETE BÝLGÝSÝ")
	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	kod = MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(1)
	kodad = MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD(1)
	musteri = MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
	musterad = MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD(1)
	sipno = MMPS10E.MUSTERISIPINO(1)
	sipartno = MMPS10E.MUSTERISIPIartNO(1)
	sipmiktar = Doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK40T", "SF_MIKTAR", sipartno, "ARTNO")
	testarih = MMPS10E.URETIMDENTESTARIH(1)
	mpsno = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO(1)
	miktar = MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(1)
	not1 = MMPS10E.NOTLAR_1(1)
	not2 = MMPS10E.NOTLAR_2(1)
	parti = MMPS10E.NOTLAR_1
	
	rows = Doc.SelectEQ("BOMU01E", "MAMULCODE", kod)
	Set Old = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_BOMU01E_SELECT")
	For i = 1 to rows
		
		aktif = Old.AKTIF_PASIF(i)
		If aktif = "*" then
			kons = Old.ACIKLAMA(i)
			evno = old.EVRAKNO(i)
			std = Doc.GetUDFValue("BOMU01E",evno,"STD",0)
			
		End If
	Next
		
	Gen10 = 25
	Gen11 = 65
	Gen20 = 22
	Gen21 = 26
	Gen30 = 25
	Gen31 = 45

	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Lucida Console", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(baslangic, solmargin, 6, Gen10, "Ürün Adý")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11, ": " & trim(kod) & " " & Trim(kodad))
	Blok20 = pe1.GetRight + 1

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solmargin, 6, Gen10, "Parti No")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11,": " & trim(parti))

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solmargin, 6, Gen10, "Müþteri")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11, ": " & trim(musterad))	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solmargin, 6, Gen10, "Üretilecek Miktar")
	res = pe1.Print(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, ": " & Doc.numformat_nlz(miktar,12,2))
	if MMPS10TM.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		StokMiktari = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK40T", "ARTIKEL_REF_REF_1", MMPS10TM.MUSTERISIPIARTNO, "ARTNO")
		
		if trim(StokMiktari) <> "" Then
			res = pe1.SetBold(1)
			res = pe1.Print(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, "Stokta Kalacak Miktar")
			res = pe1.Print(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1,  ": " & trim(Doc.NUmFormat_NLZ(Doc.CDbl(StokMiktari), 12, 2)))
			res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		End if
	End if
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solmargin, 6, Gen10, "Makina Adý")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11, ": ")

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solmargin, 6, Gen10, "Standart No")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11, ": " & trim(std))

	res = pe1.Write(baslangic, Blok20, satiryuksekligi, Gen20, "Konsantrasyon")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen21, ": " & trim(kons))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok20, 6, Gen20, "Sipariþ No")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen21, ": " & trim(sipno))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok20, 6, Gen20, "Sipariþ Miktarý")	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen21, ": " & Doc.numformat_nlz(sipmiktar,12,2))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok20, 6, Gen20,"Teslim Tarihi")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen21,": " & Doc.strtonordate(testarih))
	Blok30 = pe1.GetRight + 1
	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(baslangic, Blok30, 6, Gen30,"MPS No")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen31,": " & trim(mpsno))
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok30, 6, Gen30, "Ürt. Baþ. Saati")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen31,":" )
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok30, 6, Gen30,"Ürt.Baþ./Bit.Saati")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen31,":" )
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok30, 6, Gen30, "Ürt.Bit.Saati")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen31,":" )
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok30, 6, Gen30, "Form No ")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen31,": 39")
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)	
	Dim satirpozr
	sol = solmargin - 1
	If trim(MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(satirNo)) = "I" Then Exit Sub

	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic-8.6
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic-8.6
		Else
			satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
		End If
	End If
	hammaddekod = MMPS10T.TRNUM(satirno)
	hammaddekod = MMPS10T.SRNUM(satirno)
	hammaddekod1 = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirno)
	Set S00 = Doc.Select1("dwedwdasfg", "STOK00", "KOD='" & MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo) & "'", "", -1)

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		AlanDegeri = ""
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
		If AlanAdi = "?" then
			AlanDegeri = ""
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "_FORMUL" then
			if MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo) <> MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU or _
				(MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo) = MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU and UrunAgacindaVar(MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU)) _
						or Basilacak Then
				AlanDegeri = Doc.Div(MMPS10T.R_MIKTART(satirNo), MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR) * 100
				formulkg = formulkg + AlanDegeri
				res = pe1.SelectFont("Lucida Console", 12)
			End if
			solFormul = sol
			GenFormul = sutunGenisligi			
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "DM" then
			kaynakkodu = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirno)
			DM = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10T",trim(jobno),"DM",0)
			AlanDegeri = trim(DM)
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "VIDA" then
			kaynakkodu = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirno)
			vida = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10T",trim(jobno),"VIDA",0)
			AlanDegeri = trim(vida)
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "_ISI_DAYANIM" Then
			AlanDegeri = S00.B_B_TECH_5
		Else
			AlanDegeri = MMPS10T.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,SatirNo)
		End If
		If AlanAdi = "KAYNAK_AD" then
			AlanDegeri = S00.AD
			if Len(trim(AlanDegeri)) > 30 Then
				res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 9)
			End if
		End If

		If AlanAdi = "R_MIKTART" then
			toplamkg = toplamkg+AlanDegeri
			solToplam = sol
			GenToplam = sutunGenisligi
			res = pe1.SelectFont("Lucida Console", 10)
		End If
		
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			If AlanAdi = "_FORMUL" then
				ondalik = 2
			else
				Ondalik = 3
			End If
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			AlanDegeri = Doc.NumFormat(AlanDegeri, 18, ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.StrtoNordate(AlanDegeri)
		End If
		
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Sol, 6, SutunGenisligi - 2, AlanDegeri)
		
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next
End Sub
Sub EskiMPSSatirYazdir(pe1, satirNo)
	If satirNo = 1 Then
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic-8.6
	Else
		satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
	End If
	sol = solMargin - 1
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 8)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		If AlanAdi = "_ESKI_HMD" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", M10T_Old.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo), "")
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "_ESKI_FORMUL" Then
			MPSMiktari = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", M10T_Old.EVRAKNO(satirNo), "")
			AlanDegeri = Doc.Div(M10T_Old.R_MIKTART(satirNo), MPSMiktari) * 100
			res = pe1.SelectFont("Lucida Console", 8)
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "_ESKI_VIDA" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10T", M10T_Old.JOBNO(satirNo), "VIDA", 0)
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "_ESKI_DOZAJ" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10T", M10T_Old.JOBNO(satirNo), "DM", 0)
		Else
			AlanDegeri = ""
		End If
		
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = 3
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			AlanDegeri = Doc.numformat_NLZ(AlanDegeri, 18, ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.StrtoNordate(AlanDegeri)
		End If
		if trim(CStr(AlanDegeri)) <> "" Then
			res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Sol, 6, SutunGenisligi - 2, AlanDegeri)
		End if
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*6+1, solmargin, satiryuksekligi, 20, "TOPLAM :")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*6+1, solFormul, satiryuksekligi, GenFormul - 2,Doc.numformat(formulkg,12,2))
	res = pe1.Write(satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*6+1, solToplam, satiryuksekligi, GenToplam - 2,Doc.numformat(toplamkg,12,3))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	
	
	
	son2 = satirsonu+satiryuksekligi*4
	
	'DÝKEY ÇÝZGÝLER (SUTUN ÇÝZGÝLERÝ)
	For i = 0 To UBound(grid,1) - 1
		LineX = cint(xCizgi(i))
		If LineX = 0 Then Exit For
		res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 18, LineX, 44, 0)
	Next
	
	'-------satýrlar için cerceve ve cizgiler
	res = pe1.Box(satirBaslangic - 18, solmargin-2, 44, sagMargin - solmargin+2)
	res = pe1.Box(UstMargin + 3, eskiSol - 1, satirBaslangic - UstMargin - 21, sagMargin - EskiSol + 1)
	
	for i = 0 to 7
		res = pe1.Line((satirBaslangic-9)+i*satiryuksekligi, solmargin-2, 0, sagMargin - solmargin+2)
	next
	
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")

	satirsonu1 = satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*8

	'---------SICAKLIKLAR
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
		
	kod = MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(1)
	rows = Doc.SelectEQ("BOMU01E", "MAMULCODE", kod)
	Set Old = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_BOMU01E_SELECT")
	For i = 1 to rows	
		aktif = Old.AKTIF_PASIF(i)
		If aktif = "*" then
			kons = Old.ACIKLAMA(i)
			evno = old.EVRAKNO(i)
			std = Doc.GetUDFValue("BOMU01E",evno,"STD",0)
		End If
	Next
	
	ReceteNotu = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "RECETE_NOT", 0)
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu1+28, solmargin+214, -2, sagMargin - solMargin - 215, ReceteNotu)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 14)
	If trim(std) = "" then
		res = pe1.Write(satirsonu1+46, solmargin+245, 7, sagMargin - solMargin - 245,"0.5 kg")
	else
		res = pe1.Write(satirsonu1+46, solmargin+245, 7, sagMargin - solMargin - 245,"0.15 kg")
	End If

	if S32E_Old.GetRowCount() > 0 Then	
		oner1 = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E", S32E_Old.EVRAKNO, "ON1", 0)
		oner2 = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E", S32E_Old.EVRAKNO, "ON2", 0)
		oner3 = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E", S32E_Old.EVRAKNO, "ON3", 0)
		oner4 = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E", S32E_Old.EVRAKNO, "ON4", 0)
		oner5 = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E", S32E_Old.EVRAKNO, "ON5", 0)
		oner6 = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E", S32E_Old.EVRAKNO, "ON6", 0)
		oner7 = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E", S32E_Old.EVRAKNO, "ON7", 0)
		oner8 = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E", S32E_Old.EVRAKNO, "ON8", 0)
		oner9 = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E", S32E_Old.EVRAKNO, "ON9", 0)
	End if

	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	Gen11 = 10
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu1 + 5.5, solMargin + 13, satiryuksekligi, Gen11, oner1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, Gen11,oner2)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, Gen11,oner3)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, Gen11,oner4)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, Gen11,oner5)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, Gen11,oner6)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, Gen11,oner7)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, Gen11,oner8)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, Gen11,oner9)
	SicaklikSag = pe1.GetRight + 1

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
'	GidaUygunluk = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU, "BI_FOOD_AP", 0)
'	If trim(GidaUygunluk) <> "" Then
'		res = pe1.StoreSet(1)

'	End If
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)

	if MMPS10TM.GetRowCount() > 0 Then

		res = pe1.NoPen()
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 8)
		BarkodNo = trim(MMPS10E.EVRAKNO) & MMPS10TM.TRNUM(1)
		BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(BarkodNo)
		res = pe1.Barcode(pe1.GetTop, SicaklikSag + 10, 13, BarkodGen, "CODE128", BarkodNo, "")
		'MSGBOX pe1.GetTop & "," & pe1.GetLeft
		res = pe1.UseSet(1)

Exit Sub
	satirsonu2 = satirsonu1+25

	Grid(8,0) = ""
	Grid(9,0) = "_ISI_DAYANIM"
	Grid(10,0) = "_ESKI_HMD"
	Grid(11,0) = "_ESKI_FORMUL"
	Grid(12,0) = "_ESKI_VIDA"
	Grid(13,0) = "_ESKI_DOZAJ"
	
	Grid(0,1) = 10
	Grid(1,1) = 11
	Grid(2,1) = 16
	Grid(3,1) = 61
	Grid(4,1) = 26
	Grid(5,1) = 26
	Grid(6,1) = 18
	Grid(7,1) = 15
	Grid(8,1) = 15
	Grid(9,1) = 14
	Grid(10,1) = 28
	Grid(11,1) = 12
	Grid(12,1) = 11
	Grid(13,1) = 10
	
	Grid(0,2) = "DZJ. MT."
	Grid(1,2) = "VÝDA TÝPÝ"
	Grid(2,2) = "FORMÜL"
	Grid(3,2) = "HAMMADDE ADI"
	Grid(4,2) = "HAMMADDE KODU"
	Grid(5,2) = "HAMMADDE PARTÝ NO"
	Grid(6,2) = "MÝKTAR"
	Grid(7,2) = "ÝADE"
	Grid(8,2) = "ÝLAVE"

	Grid(10,2) = "Hammade Adý"

	Grid(12,2) = "Vida Tipi"
	Grid(13,2) = "Dzj. Mt."

	Grid(7,4) = "C"

	Grid(11,4) = "N"
	Grid(12,4) = "C"

			AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)

End Sub
Function Basilacak()
	Basilacak = False
	Set M10TH1 = Doc.Select2G("fw4fs", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'", "", "EVRAKNO", -1)
	if M10TH1.GetRowCount = 0 Then Exit Function
	
	if Doc.Round(M10TH1.R_MIKTART(1), 3) = Doc.Round(MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, 3) Then Basilacak = True
End Function
Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function
Function UrunAgacindaVar(MamulKodu)
	UrunAgacindaVar = False
	QStr = "AKTIF_PASIF='*'&BOMREC_CODE='" & MamulKodu & "'&BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H'&BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE='" & MamulKodu & "'"
	Set B01T = Doc.Select1("bsfw4tsdg", "BOMU01T", QStr, "", -1)
	if B01T.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		UrunAgacindaVar = True
	End if
End Function

Sub PrintLabel()
	Set S25T = Doc.Select2("ssww", "STOK25T_LOG", "D_ID<>'"& Space(30) & "'", "", -1)
	if S25T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	Barkod = S25T.D_ID
	if trim(Barkod) = "" Then Barkod = STOK01.D_ID
	EtiketFormKodu = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("D7KIDSLB", "FORMKODU", Barkod, "")
	Doc.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S." & EtiketFormKodu,"PrintLabel"
End Sub

Sub PrintLabel()
	EtiketFormu = D7KIDSLB.FORMKODU
	if trim(EtiketFormu) = "" Then Exit Sub

	Doc.RunMacro0 "PFRM7S." & trim(D7KIDSLB.FORMKODU),"PrintLabel"

----------------------------------------------------------------
Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SENK_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S
Makro Kodu : ETK-MAMUL

Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, AltMargin, EtiketNo, BarkoddanMi
Sub PrintLabel1()
	BarkoddanMi = True
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub
Sub PrintForm()
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub
Sub PrintLabel()
	par1=SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME
	if BarkoddanMi Then
		Set d7LB = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.ETIKET","UretimBarkod_EtiketYarat", par1)
	Else
		Set d7LB = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.ETIKET","GetEtiketTable", par1)
	End if
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu= 60
	sayfaEni = 100

	EtiketBoyu = 60
	EtiketEni = 100
	Ustmargin = 2
	AltMargin = EtiketBoyu - 2
	EtiketlerArasi = 0
	solMargin = 2
	sagMargin = EtiketEni - 2

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket", sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
'	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
'	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)

	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRecCount()
		if i > 1 Then res=pe1.PageBreak
		Call Etiket(d7LB, pe1,i)
	Next 

	if trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) = "FX" Then
		res = pe1.Show()
	Else
		if Left(Doc.GetDocType, 6) = "_GNPRG" Then
			'res = pe1.PrintDoc(0)
			res = pe1.PrintDoc(1)
		Else
			res = pe1.PrintDoc(1)
		End if
	End if
End Sub
Sub Etiket(d7LB, pe1,satirNo)
	StokKodu = d7LB.SKOD(satirNo)
	StokAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", StokKodu, "")

'	Konsantrasyon = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", StokKodu, "BI_CONCENT", 0)
	Set B01E = Doc.Select1("svge4gfh", "BOMU01E", "AKTIF_PASIF='*'&MAMULCODE='" & D7LB.SKOD(satirNo) & "'", "", -1)
	if B01E.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		Konsantrasyon = B01E.ACIKLAMA
	End if
	LotNo = d7LB.LOTNUMBER(satirNo)
	SeriNo = d7LB.SERINO(satirNo)
	Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(satirNo)
	Birim = d7LB.SF_UNIT(satirNo)
	BarkodNo = d7LB.KOD(satirNo)
	Tarih = d7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo)
	Musteri = d7LB.CH_MUSTERI(satirNo)
	MusteriAdi = GetMusteriAdi(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", Musteri, ""))
	FoodApp = Doc.GetUDFValue("STOK00", StokKodu, "BI_FOOD_AP", 0)
	if trim(FoodApp) = "EVET" Then
		GidayaUygun = "X"
		GidayaUygunDegil = ""
	Elseif trim(FoodApp) = "HAYIR" Then
		GidayaUygunDegil = "X"
		GidayaUygun = ""
	Else
		GidayaUygunDegil = ""
		GidayaUygun = ""
	End if

	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 32)
	res = pe1.SetRotation(270)
	res = pe1.SetTextColor(RGB(120,120,120))
	res = pe1.Write(Ust, sagMargin - 15 , 60, 17, trim(D7LB.REFTEXT01(satirNo)))
'	res = pe1.Print(Ust, sagMargin - 15 , trim(D7LB.REFTEXT01(satirNo)))
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)
	res = pe1.Box(UstMargin, solMargin, AltMargin - UstMargin, sagMargin - solMargin)

	KutuEni = 85
	KutuSol = (sagMargin - solMargin - KutuEni) / 2
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 16)
	res = pe1.Box(UstMargin + 1, KutuSol, 6.5, KutuEni)
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, KutuSol, 8, KutuEni, trim(StokAdi))

	KutuEni = 57
	KutuSol = (sagMargin - solMargin - KutuEni) / 2
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 16)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Box(KutuAlt + 2, KutuSol, 8, KutuEni)
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 1, KutuSol, 8, KutuEni, trim(StokKodu))
	UstBlokAlt = KutuAlt

	sol = solMargin + 2
	KutuEni = 42
	KutuSol = sol
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 8)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Box(KutuAlt + 2, KutuSol, 6, KutuEni)
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop, KutuSol + 15, pe1.GetBottom - pe1.GetTop, 0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 1, KutuSol + 1, 5, 13, "MÜÞTERÝ :")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 5, 25, trim(MusteriAdi))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Box(KutuAlt + 2, KutuSol, 6, KutuEni)
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop, KutuSol + 15, pe1.GetBottom - pe1.GetTop, 0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 1, KutuSol + 1, 5, 13, "TARÝH :")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 5, 25, Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Box(KutuAlt + 2, KutuSol, 6, KutuEni)
	KutuSag = pe1.GetRight
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom
	EnAlt = pe1.GetBottom
'	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop, KutuSol + 15, pe1.GetBottom - pe1.GetTop, 0)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 1, KutuSol + 1, 5, 28, "KONSANTRASYON : ")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, KutuSag - pe1.GetRight - 2, "% " & Konsantrasyon)

	if trim(Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "GK_10", D7LB.CH_MUSTERI(satirNo), "")) = "E" Then GidaVar = True

	KutuUst = UstBlokAlt + 2
	KutuSol = KutuSag + 4
	KutuSag = KutuSag + 46
	ortaSol = KutuSol + 15

	res = pe1.Write(KutuUst + 1, KutuSol + 1, 5, 14, "LOT NO :")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, KutuSag - pe1.GetRight - 1, LotNo)

	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop + 5, KutuSol, 0, KutuSag - KutuSol)
	cizgi1 = pe1.GetTop
	if GidaVar Then
		res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop + 6.5, KutuSol + 15, 0, KutuSag - KutuSol - 15)
		cizgi2 = pe1.GetTop
		res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop + 6.5, KutuSol, 0, KutuSag - KutuSol)
		cizgi3 = pe1.GetTop
		res = pe1.Line(Cizgi1, KutuSol + 34, cizgi3 - Cizgi1, 0)
	
		res = pe1.Write(Cizgi1 + 5, KutuSol + 1, 5, 14, "GIDAYA")
		res = pe1.Write(Cizgi1 + 1.5, OrtaSol + 1, 5, 14, "UYGUNDUR")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, KutuSag - pe1.GetRight - 1, GidayaUygun)

		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(Cizgi2, OrtaSol + 1, 10, 14, "UYGUN DEÐÝLDÝR")
		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 1.5, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, KutuSag - pe1.GetRight - 1, GidayaUygunDegil)
	Else
		Cizgi3 = Cizgi1
	End if
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(Cizgi3 + 1, KutuSol + 1, 5, 14, "Miktar :")
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 3.5, KutuSag - pe1.GetRight - 1,Doc.NumFormat(Miktar, 20, Doc.SigDig(Miktar)) & " " & Birim)
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom
	res = pe1.Box(KutuUst, KutuSol, KutuAlt - KutuUst, KutuSag - KutuSol)
	res = pe1.Line(KutuUst, OrtaSol, KutuAlt - KutuUst, 0)

	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 8)
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(BarkodNo)
	res = pe1.Barcode(EnAlt + 1, solMargin + 10, 13, BarkodGen, "CODE128", trim(BarkodNo), "")

	Set d7PL = Doc.Select1("wef234fsdf", "D7KIDSPL", "KOD='" & d7LB.KOD(satirNo) & "'", "KOD", -1)
	if d7PL.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 7)
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin, sagMargin - 80, 5, 79, Doc.Cdbl(d7PL.D_ID(1)) & "-" & Doc.Cdbl(d7PL.D_ID(d7PL.GetRowCount)))
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	End if

	res = pe1.SetRotation(270)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin, sagMargin - 3.5, 55,5, Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun) & "-" & Doc.Saat(5))
	res = pe1.SetRotation(0)
End Sub
Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function
Function GetMusteriAdi(MusteriAdi)
	GetMusteriAdi = MusteriAdi
	Parantez = InStr(1, GetMusteriAdi, "(")
	if Parantez <= 1 Then Exit Function
	GetMusteriAdi = Left(MusteriAdi, Parantez - 1)
End Function

Form Kaynak Kodu

Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo, CHStokKodu, S69T
Dim EtiketEni, EtiketlerArasi
Sub PrintForm()
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub
Sub PrintLabel()
	STOK69T.SafePack
	Set S69T = Doc.Select2("sdf34sd", "STOK69T", "", "LOCATION1+LOCATION2+<LOCATION3", -1)
	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket", 297, 210, "LANDSCAPE")
	res = pe1.NoPen()
'	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)

	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	Ustmargin = 15
	EtiketBoyu = 57
	EtiketEni = 65
	BoydanEtiketlerArasi = 45
	EndenEtiketlerArasi = 72
	solMargin = 5

	EndenEtiketSayisi = 4
	BoydanEtiketSayisi = 4
	SayfaEtiketSayisi = EndenEtiketSayisi * BoydanEtiketSayisi

	for i = 1 To S69T.getRowCount
		EtiketNo = EtiketNo + 1
		if S69T.LOCATION1(i) & S69T.LOCATION2(i) <> EskiL1L2 Then EtiketNo = 1
		EskiL1L2 = S69T.LOCATION1(i) & S69T.LOCATION2(i)

		yEtiketSayisi = (EtiketNo-1) mod BoydanEtiketSayisi

		if yEtiketSayisi = 0 Then
			xEtiketSayisi = xEtiketSayisi + 1
		End if
		if EtiketNo mod SayfaEtiketSayisi = 1 and EtiketNo > 1 then
			res = pe1.PageBreak()
			xEtiketSayisi = 1
		End if

		Ust = Ustmargin + yEtiketSayisi * BoydanEtiketlerArasi
		sol = solmargin + (xEtiketSayisi - 1) * EndenEtiketlerArasi
		Call Etiket(pe1,i, Ust, Sol)
	Next
	res = pe1.Show()
	Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub
Sub Etiket(pe1,i, Ust, Sol)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 30)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	LokStr = trim(S69T.LOCATION1(i)) & trim(S69T.LOCATION2(i)) & trim(S69T.LOCATION3(i))
	if trim(LokStr) = "" Then
		Lokasyon = "--" & trim(S69T.AMBCODE(i))
	Else
		Lokasyon = "--" & trim(S69T.LOCATION1(i)) & "-" & trim(S69T.LOCATION2(i)) & "-" & trim(S69T.LOCATION3(i)) & "-" & trim(S69T.LOCATION4(i)) & ""

	End if
	res = pe1.Barcode(Ust,Sol+5,30,EtiketEni - 10, "CODE128", Lokasyon, "") 
End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu

Public sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, MAKROTABLET, MAKROTABLE, Gen1, Gen2, Gen11, Gen21
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic, SatirBitis, ToplamKg

Sub PrintForm()
	if not IsObject(MAKROTABLET) Then
		Set MAKROTABLE = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE")
		Set MAKROTABLET = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLET")
	End if
	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	UstMargin = 15
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 10
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 15
	satiryuksekligi = 7
	siraNo = 0
	ToplamKg = 0
	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()	
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Premix Recetesi",SayfaEni,SayfaBoyu,"LANDSCAPE")
	
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To MAKROTABLET.GetRowCount()
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	
	res = pe1.Show()
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	Gen1 = 57
	Gen2 = 40
	Gen11 = 65
	Gen21 = 25

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 14)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin, solMargin, 7, Gen1, "MAKÝNA ADI")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, Gen2, ": ")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 7, Gen1, "MPS")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, Gen2, ": " & trim(MAKROTABLE.MPSNO))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 7, Gen1, "ÜRÜN KODU")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, Gen2, ": " & trim(MAKROTABLE.MAMULSTOKKODU))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 7, Gen1, "LOT NO")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, Gen2, ": " & trim(MAKROTABLE.LOTNUMBER))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 7, Gen1, "ÜRETÝM MÝKTARI")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, Gen2, ": " & MAKROTABLE.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR & " " & MAKROTABLE.SF_SF_UNIT)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 7, Gen1, "ÜRETÝM BAÞLANGIÇ SAATÝ")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, Gen2, ": ")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 7, Gen1, "ÜRETÝM BÝTÝÞ SAATÝ")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, Gen2, ": ")

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 7, Gen11 + Gen21 + 1, "ÖN KARIÞIM MÝKTARLARI")
	satirBaslangic = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi

End Sub
Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)	
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	if siraNo > 15 Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		siraNo = 0
	End if
	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	satirPozr = satirBaslangic + (siraNo - 1) * satirYuksekligi
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, solMargin + 1, 7, Gen11 - 2, MAKROTABLET.R_KAYNAKKODU_STOK00_AD(satirNo))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, Gen21 - 2, Doc.NumFormat(MAKROTABLET.PREMIX_MIKTARI(satirNo), 12,3))
	ToplamKg = ToplamKg + MAKROTABLET.PREMIX_MIKTARI(satirNo)
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.5)
	KutuUst = satirBaslangic - satirYuksekligi - 1
	res = pe1.Box(KutuUst, solMargin, pe1.GetBottom - KutuUst, Gen11 + Gen21 + 2)
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom
	KutuSag = pe1.GetRight
	res = pe1.Line(KutuUst + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 0, KutuSag - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + Gen11 + 1, KutuAlt - pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, 0)

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)

	For i = 1 to MAKROTABLET.GetRowCount() - 1
		res = pe1.Line(KutuUst + (i + 1) * satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 0, KutuSag - solMargin)
	Next
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Box(KutuAlt, solMargin, satirYuksekligi, KutuSag - solMargin)
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom
	satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + 1
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, solMargin + 1, 7, Gen11 - 2, "TOPLAM (kg)")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, Gen21 - 2, Doc.NumFormat_NLZ(ToplamKg, 12,3))

	res = pe1.Box(KutuAlt, solMargin, satirYuksekligi, KutuSag - solMargin)

	satirPozr = pe1.GetTop + 1
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, solMargin + 1, 7, Gen11 - 2, "ÖN KARIÞIM GEÇÝÞ SAYISI")


End Sub

Public sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, M10T, MTable1, Gen1, Gen2, Gen11, Gen21
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic, SatirBitis, ToplamKg

Sub PrintForm()
	Set MTable1 = Doc.GetTableObject("MAKROTABLE1")

	if Doc.GetUDFValue("BOMU01E", MTable1.UAGAC_FOY_NO, "PREMIX_CKS", 0) = 0 Then
		Doc.d7MsgBox "Premix çýkýþ miktarý girilmemiþ", "Ýþlem Kesildi", MB_OK
		Exit Sub
	End if

	Set M10T = Doc.Select1("sef23ff", "MMPS10T", "EVRAKNO='" & MTable1.MPSNO2 & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'", "", -1)
	if M10T.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub
	Set B01T = Doc.Select1("ef23fd", "BOMU01T", "EVRAKNO='" & MTable1.UAGAC_FOY_NO & "'&BOMREC_INPUTTYPE='H'", "", -1)
	For i = 1 to B01T.GetRowCOunt()
		satirNo = M10T.AddRow()
		M10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(satirNo) = "H"
		M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo) = B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAKCODE(i)
		M10T.R_MIKTAR0(satirNo) = -1 * B01T.BOMREC_KAYNAK0(i)
	Next
	satirNo = M10T.AddRow()
	M10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(satirNo) = "H"
	M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo) = "ÖN KARIÞIM"
	M10T.R_MIKTAR0(satirNo) = MTable1.UAGAC_MIKTARI
	M10T.GroupBy2("R_KAYNAKKODU")


	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	UstMargin = 15
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 10
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 15
	satiryuksekligi = 7
	siraNo = 0
	ToplamKg = 0
	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()	
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Üretim Formülü",SayfaEni,SayfaBoyu,"LANDSCAPE")
	
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To M10T.GetRowCount()
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	
	res = pe1.Show()
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	Gen1 = 57
	Gen2 = 50
	Gen11 = 83
	Gen21 = 25

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 14)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin, solMargin, 7, Gen1 + Gen2 + 1, "PRE-MIX ÜRETÝM BÝLGÝLERÝ")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 7, Gen1, "BAÞLAMA TARÝH - SAAT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, Gen2, ": ....../....../......... - ...... : ......")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 7, Gen1, "BÝTÝÞ TARÝH - SAAT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, Gen2, ": ....../....../......... - ...... : ......")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 7, Gen1 + 3, "SICAKLIK BÖLGELERÝ")
	KutuEni = Gen2 / 6
	For i = 1 to 6
		res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, SatirYuksekligi, KutuEni)
	Next
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 2 * satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 7, Gen11 + Gen21 + 1, "ÜRETÝM FORMÜLÜ (%)")
	satirBaslangic = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi

End Sub
Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)	
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	if siraNo > 15 Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		siraNo = 0
	End if
	siraNo = siraNo + 1
	satirPozr = satirBaslangic + (siraNo - 1) * satirYuksekligi
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	StokAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo), "")
	if trim(StokAdi) = "" Then StokAdi = M10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo)
	MPSMiktari = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", MTable1.MPSNO2, "")
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, solMargin + 1, 7, Gen11 - 2, StokAdi)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	Oran = Doc.Div(M10T.R_MIKTAR0(satirNo), MPSMiktari) * 100
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, Gen21 - 2, Doc.NumFormat(Oran, 8,2))
	ToplamKg = ToplamKg + M10T.R_MIKTAR0(satirNo)
End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.5)
	KutuUst = satirBaslangic - satirYuksekligi - 1
	res = pe1.Box(KutuUst, solMargin, pe1.GetBottom - KutuUst, Gen11 + Gen21 + 2)
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom
	KutuSag = pe1.GetRight
	res = pe1.Line(KutuUst + satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 0, KutuSag - solMargin)
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + Gen11 + 1, KutuAlt - pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, 0)

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)

	For i = 1 to M10T.GetRowCount() - 1
		res = pe1.Line(KutuUst + (i + 1) * satirYuksekligi, solMargin, 0, KutuSag - solMargin)
	Next
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Box(KutuAlt, solMargin, satirYuksekligi, KutuSag - solMargin)

	satirPozr = KutuAlt + 1
	MPSMiktari = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", MTable1.MPSNO2, "")
	Oran = Doc.Div(ToplamKg, MPSMiktari) * 100

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, solMargin + 1, 7, Gen11 - 2, "TOPLAM (%)")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, pe1.GetRight + 2, 7, Gen21 - 2, Doc.NumFormat(Oran, 12,2))

End Sub

Form Kaynak Kodu

Makro Kodu : ETK-HURDA

Public Ust, EtiketBoyu, UstMargin, Solmargin, sagMargin, EtiketNo
Sub PrintForm()
	Call PrintLabel()
End Sub
Sub PrintLabel()
	par1=SC_SCRIPTMODULEFILENAME
	Set d7LB = Doc.RunMacro1("MAKR7S.ETIKET","GetEtiketTable", par1)
	if d7LB.GetRowCount() = 0 Then Exit Sub

	set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	EtiketNo = 0
	sayfaBoyu= 60
	sayfaEni = 100

	Ustmargin = 3
	EtiketBoyu = 60
	EtiketEni = 100
	EtiketlerArasi = 0
	solMargin = 2
	sagMargin = EtiketEni - 2

	res = pe1.Open(0, "Etiket", sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, "")
	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAlignment(2)
'	res = pe1.SetBarcodeParms(2,2)
'	res = pe1.SetBarcodeAutoCheckSum(0)

	For i = 1 to d7LB.GetRecCount()
		if i > 1 Then res=pe1.PageBreak
		Call Etiket(d7LB, pe1,i)
	Next 

	if trim(Doc.Getd7UserName) = "FX" Then
		res = pe1.Show()
	Else
		if Left(Doc.GetDocType, 6) = "_GNPRG" Then
			res = pe1.PrintDoc(0)
		Else
			res = pe1.PrintDoc(1)
		End if
	End if
End Sub
Sub Etiket(d7LB, pe1,satirNo)
	Ust = Ustmargin
	Alt = Ust + EtiketBoyu

	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 30)
	res = pe1.SetRotation(270)
	res = pe1.SetTextColor(RGB(120,120,120))
	res = pe1.Write(Ust, sagMargin - 14 , 54, 14, "HURDA")
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)

	StokKodu = d7LB.SKOD(satirNo)
	StokAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", StokKodu, "")
	LotNo = d7LB.LOTNUMBER(satirNo)
	SeriNo = d7LB.SERINO(satirNo)
	Miktar = d7LB.SF_BAKIYE(satirNo)
	Birim = d7LB.SF_UNIT(satirNo)
	BarkodNo = d7LB.KOD(satirNo)
	Tarih = d7LB.REFTARIH1(satirNo)
	Tedarikci = d7LB.CH_TEDARIKCI(satirNo)
	TedarikciAdi = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("CARI00", "AD", Tedarikci, "")

	Aralik = 5.5
	Aralik1 = 10
	Basla = Ust + 22
	Genislik = 37
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 20)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin, solMargin, 20, sagMargin - solMargin, Trim(StokAdi))

	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 18)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 15, solMargin, 10, sagMargin - solMargin, trim(StokKodu))

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 15)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik1, solMargin + 2, 15, Genislik, "LOT NO")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 15, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & LotNo)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 15)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, solMargin + 2, 10, Genislik, "MÝKTAR")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 10, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & Doc.NumFormat(Miktar, 20, Doc.SigDig(Miktar)) & " " & Birim)

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 15)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, solMargin + 2, 8, Genislik, "GÝRÝÞ TARÝHÝ")
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 8, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(Tarih))

'	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 15)
'	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + Aralik, solMargin + 2, 8, Genislik, "ÝRSALÝYE NO")
'	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
'	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 8, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 1, ": " & trim(D7LB.EVRAKNO(satirNo)))

	res = pe1.NoPen()
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 8)
	BarkodGen = Barkodgenisligi(BarkodNo)
	res = pe1.Barcode(pe1.GetTop + Aralik + 1, solMargin + 5, 13, BarkodGen, "CODE128", trim(BarkodNo), "")

	res = pe1.SetRotation(270)
	res = pe1.Write(UstMargin, sagMargin - 3.5, 55,5, Doc.StrToNorDate(Doc.BuGun) & "-" & Doc.Saat(5))
	res = pe1.SetRotation(0)
End Sub

Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function

Form Kaynak Kodu
Firma Kodu : SENK_2014 / Uygulama : PFRM7S

Public sayfaTuru, sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu, ToplamEn , BasilabilirAlan
Public siraNo, satirBaslangic,Tip
Public AlanSayisi()		 
Public xCizgi()
Public Secilenler()
Public Grid
Sub PrintForm()
	ReDim AlanSayisi(100)		 
	ReDim xCizgi(100)
	ReDim Secilenler(100,3)
'	Set xCizgi = Nothing
	ToplamEn = 0
	BasilabilirAlan = 0
	siraNo = 0
	satirBaslangic = 0
                      
	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
             
	Set pe1 = doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()

	Call Alanlar()
		
	Tip = "LANDSCAPE"
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Stok Kod-Lot-Seri Transfer Fiþi", Sayfaeni, Sayfaboyu, Tip)
		

	BasilabilirAlan = SayfaEni - 20
	satirSonu = sayfaBoyu - 30

	satirBaslangic = 70

	Dim pe1
        siraNo = 0        
        Dim satirSayisi_tableT
        satirSayisi_tableT = STOK25T.GetRecCount()
        Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
        For I = 1 To satirSayisi_tableT
                Call Print_form_satir(pe1, I)
        Next
        
        Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

        res = pe1.Show()
        res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
      res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
        res = pe1.nopen()
        res = pe1.settransparentmode(0)
        res = pe1.box(8, 8, 30, SayfaEni-110)
        res = pe1.UseSet(1001)
        res = pe1.RemoveSet(1001)
        
        res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
        res = pe1.nopen()
		If Tip = "LANDSCAPE" Then
	    	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 14)
     		res = pe1.setbold(1)
	       	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
     	   	res = pe1.write(15, 10, 15, SayfaEni - 20, "Stok Kod-Lot-Seri Transfer Fiþi")
       	   	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	       	res = pe1.Picture(10, 12, 16, -2, Doc.Getd7Directory_DataFirm & trim(Doc.Customp_Gets("!LOGO")), 114)
		Else
	       	res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 12)
     	   	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	       	res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
     	   	res = pe1.write(18, 10, 15, SayfaEni - 20, "Stok Kod-Lot-Seri Transfer Fiþi")
       	   	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	       	res = pe1.Picture(11, 10, 10, 35, "\d24\images\muhtese.jpg", 18)
		End If
        res = pe1.selectfont("Helvetica", "12")
        res = pe1.setbold(1)
        res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
'        res = pe1.write(15.8, 18, 10, 45, firma)
        res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", "10")

        res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
'*******************
		BBasla = 32
		SatirAra = 6
		
		Tarih = doc.StrtoNordate(STOK25E.TARIH(1))
		AmbarKodu = STOK25E.AMBCODE(1)
		AmbarAdi = doc.GetDBFieldValue("GDEF00","AD",AmbarKodu,"")  
		HAmbarKodu = STOK25E.TARGETAMBCODE(1)
		HAmbarAdi = doc.GetDBFieldValue("GDEF00","AD",HAmbarKodu,"")  		
		NitelikKodu = STOK25E.NITELIK(1)  
		Nitelik = doc.GetGecoValue("STKNIT","AD",NitelikKodu ,-1)


		res = pe1.write(BBasla , 12, 6, 33, "Fiþ No")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, 45, 6, SayfaEni - 121, ": " & trim(STOK25E.EVRAKNO))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, 12, 6, 33, "Tarih")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, 45, 6, SayfaEni - 121, ": " & Tarih)
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, 12, 6, 33, "Ambar ")
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, 45, 6, SayfaEni - 100, ": " & trim(AmbarKodu) & "  " & Trim(AmbarAdi) & " >> " & _
						trim(HAmbarKodu) & "  " & Trim(HAmbarAdi))
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, 12, 6, 33, "Nitelik")		
		res = pe1.write(pe1.GetTop, 45, 6, SayfaEni - 121, ": " & Trim(NitelikKodu) & "  " & trim(Nitelik))
		
'***********************		
		Oran = BasilabilirAlan / ToplamEn

		res = pe1.setalign("CENTER")
		FontBoyu = FontAyarla(BasilabilirAlan,ToplamEn,10)
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", FontBoyu)
		Sol = 12
		For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
			SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1) / 3.22 * Oran
			Baslik = Grid(i,2)
			If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then	
				res = pe1.write(satirBaslangic - 13, Sol, 10, SutunGenisligi - 4 , Baslik)
				Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
			End If
		Next

		res = pe1.pensize(0.6)

'yatay çizgiler
		res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
		res = pe1.Line(28, 10, 0, SayfaEni-20)
		res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 14, 10, 0, sayfaEni -20)
		res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 2, 10, 0, sayfaEni -20)
		res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
		res = pe1.PenColor(RGB(0, 0, 0))        
		res = pe1.pensize(0.5)
		Dim satiryuksekligi, x
		satiryuksekligi = 6

        Call Normal(pe1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)
Dim satirpozr, satiryuksekligi
        satiryuksekligi = 6
        sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
        If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
                Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
                siraNo = 1
                res = pe1.PageBreak()
                Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
                satirpozr = satirBaslangic
        Else
                If satirNo = 1 Then
                        satirpozr = satirBaslangic
                Else
                        satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi+3
                End If
                siraNo = siraNo + 1
        End If
			eskikod=STOK25T.KOD(satirno)
     	yenikod=STOK25T.NEWCODE(satirno)
			tarih=STOK25E.TARIH(1)
			ambar=STOK25E.AMBCODE(1)
			evrakno=STOK25E.EVRAKNO(1)
			trnum=STOK25T.TRNUM(satirno)

		

   
        res = pe1.StoreSet(1001)
		FontBoyu = FontAyarla(BasilabilirAlan,ToplamEn,10)
		res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", FontBoyu)
        res = pe1.setbold(0)
        Sol = 12
        res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
			For i=0 To UBound(Grid,1)
				SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)  / 3.22 * BasilabilirAlan / ToplamEn
				DegiskenAdi = Grid(i,0)
'				If DegiskenAdi = "" Then Exit For
				Degisken = trim(cstr(STOK25T.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,SatirNo)))
				res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
				If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
					res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
					Ondalik = Grid(i,6)
					Degisken = doc.numformat(Degisken, grid(i,1),Ondalik)
				Elseif Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
					Degisken = Doc.StrtoNordate(Degisken)
				End If
				If SutunGenisligi > 0 Then
					res = pe1.write(satirpozr, Sol, satiryuksekligi, SutunGenisligi - 4, Degisken)
					Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
					xCizgi(j) =	Sol - 2
					j = j + 1
				End If	
			Next			
         
     		res = pe1.setbold(0)

        sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()

End Sub
Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
	res = pe1.pensize(0.6)
'dýþ çerçeve
	res = pe1.box(8, 10, satirSonu - 3, SayfaEni - 20)

'dikey çizgiler
	res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
	For i = 0 To UBound(xCizgi) - 2
		LineX = cint(xCizgi(i))
		res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 14, LineX, satirSonu - satirBaslangic + 19, 0)
	Next
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(7,7)
	Grid(0,0) = "KOD"
	Grid(1,0) = "KOD_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "LOTNUMBER"
	Grid(3,0) = "NEWCODE"
	Grid(4,0) = "NEWCODE_STOK00_AD"
	Grid(5,0) = "NEWLOTNUMBER"
	Grid(6,0) = "SF_MIKTAR"
	Grid(7,0) = "OSF_SF_UNIT"

	Grid(0,1) = 35	
	Grid(1,1) = 70
	Grid(2,1) = 25
	Grid(3,1) = 35		
	Grid(4,1) = 70
	Grid(5,1) = 18	
	Grid(6,1) = 20		
	Grid(7,1) = 14

	Grid(0,2) = "Stok Kodu"				
	Grid(1,2) = "Stok Adý"				
	Grid(2,2) = "Lot No"				
	Grid(3,2) = "Yeni Stok Kodu"				
	Grid(4,2) = "Yeni Stok Adý"		
	Grid(5,2) = "Yeni Lot No"				
	Grid(6,2) = "Miktar"				
	Grid(7,2) = "Birim"				
	
	Grid(0,4) = "C"	
	Grid(1,4) = "C"	
	Grid(2,4) = "C"	
	Grid(3,4) = "C"	
	Grid(4,4) = "C"	
	Grid(5,4) = "C"	
	Grid(6,4) = "N"	
	Grid(7,4) = "C"	

	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		ToplamEn = ToplamEn + Grid(i,1)
	Next
	ToplamEn = ToplamEn / 3.22
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4)="N" Then
			Degisken = Grid(i,0)
			ondalik = DecimalHesapla(Degisken)	
			Grid(i,6) = Ondalik		
		End If
	Next	
End Sub
Sub Normal(pe1)
        res = pe1.setbold(0)
        res = pe1.setitalic(0)
        res = pe1.pensize(0.4)
        res = pe1.setbkgcolor(RGB(255, 255, 255))
        res = pe1.setrotation(0)
        res = pe1.settransparentmode(1)
        res = pe1.settextcolor(RGB(0, 0, 0))
        res = pe1.selectfont("Arial", 10)
        res = pe1.setalign("left")
End Sub
Function FontAyarla(BasilacakAlan,ToplamGen,Font)
	Oran = BasilacakAlan / ToplamGen
	If Oran >= 1 Then
		FontAyarla = Font
	Else
		If oran <=0.8 Then
			FontAyarla = Round(0.8 * Font,0)
		Else
			FontAyarla = Round(Oran * Font,0)
		End If
	End If
End Function
Function SifirEkle(Sayi, OndalikHane)
    Sayi = CStr(Sayi)
    If OndalikHane - Len(Sayi) > 0 Then
        SifirEkle = Sayi & String(OndalikHane - Len(Sayi), "0")
    Else
        SifirEkle = Sayi
    End If
End Function
Function SifirSil(Sayi)
    Sayi = Trim(CStr(Sayi))
    While Right(Sayi, 1) = "0"
        Sayi = Left(Sayi, Len(Sayi) - 1)
    Wend
    SifirSil = Sayi
End Function
Function VirgulEkle(Sayi)
    If Sayi = 0 Then Exit Function
    Sayi = CStr(Sayi)
    k = Len(Sayi)
    For i = 0 To k - 1
        If i > 0 And i Mod 3 = 0 Then
            Sagdan = "," & Sagdan
        End If
        Sagdan = Mid(Sayi, k - i, 1) & Sagdan
    Next
    VirgulEkle = Sagdan
End Function
Function DecimalSayisi(Sayi)
	sayi = Cstr(Sayi)
	Nokta = InStr(1, Sayi, ".")
	If Nokta = 0 Then
		DecimalSayisi = 0
	Else
		ondalik = Right(CStr(Sayi), Len(CStr(Sayi)) - Nokta)
		DecimalSayisi = len(SifirSil(Ondalik))
	End If
End Function
Function DecimalHesapla(Degisken)
k = 0
DecCount = 0
	For i=1 To STOK25T.GetRecCount()
		DecCount = DecimalSayisi(STOK25T.GetFieldValue(Degisken,i))
		If DecCount >= k Then
			k = DecCount
		End If
	Next
DecimalHesapla = k
End Function
Function NumerikMi(DegiskenAdi)
	For i = 1 To STOK25T.GetRecCount()
		k = Table.GetFieldValue(DegiskenAdi,i)
		If Not(Isnumeric(k)) Then
			NumerikMi = 0
			Exit For
		End If
	NumerikMi=1
	Next
End Function

Public sayfaBoyu, sayfaEni, sonSatir, satirSonu, kutuSag, eskiSol, S32E_Old, M10T_Old, KontrolSol, KontrolUst, Gradesol
Public UstMargin, SolMargin, SagMargin, AltMargin, satiryuksekligi, satirAlt

Sub PrintForm()
	QStr = "STOK_CODE='" & MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU & "'&RECTYPE<>'D'&RECTYPE<>'P'&SF_MIKTAR>0&EVRAKNO<>'" & MMPS10E.EVRAKNO & "'"
	Set S32E_Old = Doc.Select1("fsefs4f4fsf", "SFDC32E", QStr, "<RECTARIH1",1)
	If S32E_Old.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		QStr = "EVRAKNO='" & S32E_Old.MPSNO(1) & "'&R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'&R_KAYNAKKODU<>'" & MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU & "'"
		Set M10T_Old = Doc.Select1("sef43ffefeg", "MMPS10T", QStr, "", -1)
	End If
	Dim pe1
	sayfaBoyu = 210
	sayfaEni = 297
	UstMargin = 5
	AltMargin = SayfaBoyu - 10
	SolMargin = 10
	SagMargin = SayfaEni - 15
	satiryuksekligi = 4.3
	satirBaslangic = 50
	satirsonu = satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*27
	toplamkg = 0
	formulkg = 0
	std = ""
	Set pe1 = Doc.CreatePrintEngineOutput()	
	res = pe1.Open(0, "Uretim Takip Formu",SayfaEni,SayfaBoyu,"LANDSCAPE")
	

	CorrIndex = 0
	For i = 1 to 9
		if Doc.Cdbl(Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10T", MMPS10T.JOBNO(1), "COR_RCTMIK", i)) = 0 Then Exit For
		CorrIndex = i
	Next

	Call Alanlar()
	
	MMPS10T.SafePack
	satirSayisi = MMPS10T.GetRecCount()
	Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	For i = 1 To satirSayisi
		Call Print_form_satir(pe1, i)
	Next

	if S32E_Old.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		if M10T_Old.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
			For i = 1 to M10T_Old.GetRowCount()
				Call EskiMPSSatirYazdir(pe1, i)
			Next
		End if
	End if
	Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
'	res = pe1.SetDevPaperFormat(-2)
	
	res = pe1.Show()
	res = Doc.ActivateAfterMacro(1)
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
	baslangic = ustmargin+3
	satirAra = 4
	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	ReDim Preserve xCizgi(0)
	sol = solmargin - 1
	
	eskiSol = 0
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If eskiSol = 0 and Left(Grid(i,0), 5) = "_ESKI" Then eskiSol = sol
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		Baslik = Grid(i,2)
		res = pe1.Write(satirBaslangic - 17, Sol, 15, SutunGenisligi, Baslik)
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
		xCizgi(UBound(xCizgi)) = Sol - 1
		ReDim Preserve xCizgi(Ubound(xCizgi)+1)
	Next

	If S32E_Old.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		OncekiMakina = S32E_Old.X_T_ISMERKEZI
		AlinanHmd = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_Old.EVRAKNO,"INPUT",0)
		IadeEdilenHmd = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_Old.EVRAKNO,"IADE",0)
		KullanilanHammadde = AlinanHmd - IadeEdilenHmd

		MakinaBitisSaati = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_Old.EVRAKNO,"ME",0)
		MakinaBaslangicSaati = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_Old.EVRAKNO,"MB",0)
		UretimSuresi = MakinaBitisSaati - MakinaBaslangicSaati

		UretimHizi = Doc.Div(KullanilanHammadde, UretimSuresi)
		UretimSonucu = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E", S32E_Old.EVRAKNO, "LBC", 0)
		OncekiMPSNo = S32E_Old.MPSNO
		OncekiUretimTarihi = S32E_Old.RECTARIH1
	End if
	res = pe1.Write(Baslangic, eskiSol, 5, sagMargin - eskiSol - 1, "Önceki Üretim Bilgileri")
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	Gen40 = 30
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, eskiSol, 5, Gen40, "Kullanýlan Makina")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & trim(OncekiMakina))	

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, eskiSol, 5, Gen40, "Üretim Hýzý (kg/saat)")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & Doc.NumFormat(UretimHizi, 12, 2))	

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, eskiSol, 5, Gen40, "Üretim Sonucu (A/B)")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & UretimSonucu)	

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, eskiSol, 5, Gen40, "Önceki MPS No")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & trim(OncekiMPSNo))	

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, eskiSol, 5, Gen40, "Önceki Üretim Tarihi")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, sagMargin - pe1.GetRight - 2, ": " & Doc.StrToNorDate(OncekiUretimTarihi))	


	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",12)
	res = pe1.Write(Baslangic, solmargin+100, 6, 70, "CORRECTION REÇETE BÝLGÝSÝ")
	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	kod = MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(1)
	kodad = MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU_STOK00_AD(1)
	musteri = MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU(1)
	musterad = MMPS10E.MUSTERIKODU_CARI00_AD(1)
	sipno = MMPS10E.MUSTERISIPINO(1)
	sipartno = MMPS10E.MUSTERISIPIartNO(1)
	sipmiktar = Doc.getdbfieldvalue("STOK40T", "SF_MIKTAR", sipartno, "ARTNO")
	testarih = MMPS10E.URETIMDENTESTARIH(1)
	mpsno = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO(1)
	miktar = MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR(1)
	not1 = MMPS10E.NOTLAR_1(1)
	not2 = MMPS10E.NOTLAR_2(1)
	parti = MMPS10E.NOTLAR_1
	
	rows = Doc.SelectEQ("BOMU01E", "MAMULCODE", kod)
	Set Old = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_BOMU01E_SELECT")
	For i = 1 to rows
		
		aktif = Old.AKTIF_PASIF(i)
		If aktif = "*" then
			kons = Old.ACIKLAMA(i)
			evno = old.EVRAKNO(i)
			std = Doc.GetUDFValue("BOMU01E",evno,"STD",0)
			
		End If
	Next
		
	Gen10 = 25
	Gen11 = 45
	Gen20 = 22
	Gen21 = 21
	Gen30 = 25
	Gen31 = 45

	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Lucida Console", 12)
	res = pe1.Write(baslangic, solmargin, 6, Gen10, "Ürün Adý")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, 70, ": " & trim(kod) & " " & Trim(kodad))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solmargin, 6, Gen10, "Parti No")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11,": " & trim(parti))
	Blok20 = pe1.GetRight + 1

	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solmargin, 6, Gen10, "Müþteri")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11, ": " & trim(musterad))	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solmargin, 6, Gen10, "Üretilecek Miktar")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11, ": " & Doc.numformat_nlz(miktar,12,2))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solmargin, 6, Gen10, "Makina Adý")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11, ": ")

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, solmargin, 6, Gen10, "Standart No")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen11, ": " & trim(std))

	res = pe1.Write(baslangic + 2 * satirAra, Blok20, satiryuksekligi, Gen20, "Konsantrasyon")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen21, ": " & trim(kons))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok20, 6, Gen20, "Sipariþ No")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen21, ": " & trim(sipno))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok20, 6, Gen20, "Sipariþ Miktarý")	
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen21, ": " & Doc.numformat_nlz(sipmiktar,12,2))

	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok20, 6, Gen20,"Teslim Tarihi")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen21,": " & Doc.strtonordate(testarih))
	Blok30 = pe1.GetRight + 1
	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(baslangic + 2 * satirAra, Blok30, 6, Gen30,"MPS No")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen31,": " & trim(mpsno))
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok30, 6, Gen30, "Ürt. Baþ. Saati")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen31,":" )
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok30, 6, Gen30,"Ürt.Baþ./Bit.Saati")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen31,":" )
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirAra, Blok30, 6, Gen30, "Ürt.Bit.Saati")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 6, Gen31,":" )
End Sub

Sub Print_form_satir(pe1, satirNo)	
	Dim satirpozr
	sol = solmargin - 1
	If trim(MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKTYPE(satirNo)) = "I" Then Exit Sub

	sonSatir = pe1.GetBottom()
	If sonSatir >= satirSonu Then
		Call Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)
		res = pe1.PageBreak()
		Call Print_form_sayfabasi(pe1)
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic-8.6
	Else
		If satirNo = 1 Then
			satirpozr = satirBaslangic-8.6
		Else
			satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
		End If
	End If
	hammaddekod = MMPS10T.TRNUM(satirno)
	hammaddekod = MMPS10T.SRNUM(satirno)
	hammaddekod1 = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirno)
	Set S00 = Doc.Select1("dwedwdasfg", "STOK00", "KOD='" & MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo) & "'", "", -1)

	if CorrIndex = 0 Then
		HammaddeMiktari = MMPS10T.R_MIKTART(satirNo)
	Else	
		HammaddeMiktari = Doc.Cdbl(Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10T", MMPS10T.JOBNO(satirNo), "COR_RCTMIK", CorrIndex))
	End if
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		AlanDegeri = ""
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
		If AlanAdi = "?" then
			AlanDegeri = ""
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "_FORMUL" then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.Round(Doc.Div(HammaddeMiktari, MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR) * 100, 2)
			formulkg = formulkg + AlanDegeri
			res = pe1.SelectFont("Lucida Console", 12)
			solFormul = sol
			GenFormul = sutunGenisligi			
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "DM" then
			kaynakkodu = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirno)
			DM = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10T",trim(jobno),"DM",0)
			AlanDegeri = trim(DM)
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "VIDA" then
			kaynakkodu = MMPS10T.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirno)
			vida = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10T",trim(jobno),"VIDA",0)
			AlanDegeri = trim(vida)
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "_ISI_DAYANIM" Then
			AlanDegeri = S00.B_B_TECH_5
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "_R_MIKTART" Then
			AlanDegeri = HammaddeMiktari
		Else
			AlanDegeri = MMPS10T.GetFieldValue(AlanAdi,SatirNo)
		End If
		If AlanAdi = "KAYNAK_AD" then
			AlanDegeri = S00.AD
			if Len(trim(AlanDegeri)) > 30 Then
				res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 9)
			End if
		End If


		If AlanAdi = "_R_MIKTART" then
			toplamkg = toplamkg + AlanDegeri
			solToplam = sol
			GenToplam = sutunGenisligi
			res = pe1.SelectFont("Lucida Console", 10)
		End If
		
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = 3
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			AlanDegeri = Doc.numformat_NLZ(AlanDegeri, 18, ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.StrtoNordate(AlanDegeri)
		End If
		res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Sol, 6, SutunGenisligi - 2, AlanDegeri)
		
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next
	satirAlt = pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi
End Sub

Sub EskiMPSSatirYazdir(pe1, satirNo)
	If satirNo = 1 Then
		satirpozr = satirBaslangic-8.6
	Else
		satirpozr = pe1.GetTop() + satiryuksekligi
	End If
	sol = solMargin - 1
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial Narrow", 8)
		SutunGenisligi = Grid(i,1)
		AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
		If AlanAdi = "_ESKI_HMD" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("STOK00", "AD", M10T_Old.R_KAYNAKKODU(satirNo), "")
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "_ESKI_FORMUL" Then
			MPSMiktari = Doc.GetDBFieldValue("MMPS10E", "SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR", M10T_Old.EVRAKNO(satirNo), "")
			AlanDegeri = Doc.Div(M10T_Old.R_MIKTART(satirNo), MPSMiktari) * 100
			res = pe1.SelectFont("Lucida Console", 8)
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "_ESKI_VIDA" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10T", M10T_Old.JOBNO(satirNo), "VIDA", 0)
		ElseIf AlanAdi = "_ESKI_DOZAJ" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10T", M10T_Old.JOBNO(satirNo), "DM", 0)
		Else
			AlanDegeri = ""
		End If
		
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			Ondalik = 3
			res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
			AlanDegeri = Doc.numformat_NLZ(AlanDegeri, 18, ondalik)
		ElseIf Grid(i,4) = "D" Then
			AlanDegeri = Doc.StrtoNordate(AlanDegeri)
		End If
		if trim(CStr(AlanDegeri)) <> "" Then
			res = pe1.Write(satirpozr, Sol, 6, SutunGenisligi - 2, AlanDegeri)
		End if
		Sol = Sol + SutunGenisligi
	Next
End Sub

Sub Print_form_sayfasonu(pe1)

	res = pe1.setbold(1)
		
	CizgiYuksekligi = satirAlt - satirBaslangic + 18
	For i = 0 To UBound(grid,1)
		LineX = xCizgi(i)
		If LineX = 0 Then Exit For
		if InStr(1, Grid(i,0), "_ESKI") = 0 Then
			Yukseklik = CizgiYuksekligi
		Else
			Yukseklik = 44
		End if
		res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 18, LineX, Yukseklik, 0)
		if InStr(1, Grid(i,0), "_ESKI") = 0 Then sag2 = pe1.GetRight
	Next
	sag = pe1.GetRight
	'-------satýrlar için cerceve ve cizgiler
	res = pe1.Box(satirBaslangic - 18, solmargin-2, CizgiYuksekligi, sag2 - solmargin+2)
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom
	res = pe1.Box(UstMargin + 3, eskiSol - 1, satirBaslangic - UstMargin - 21, sagMargin - EskiSol + 1)
	
	for i = 0 to 100
		LinePoz = satirBaslangic - 9 + i * satirYuksekligi 
		if LinePoz > (KutuAlt - satirYuksekligi) Then Exit For
		res = pe1.Line(LinePoz, solmargin-2, 0, sag2 - solmargin+2)
	next
	KutuSag = sag


	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
'	res = pe1.Write(KutuAlt + 1, solmargin, satiryuksekligi, 20, "TOPLAM :")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(KutuAlt + 1, solFormul, satiryuksekligi, GenFormul - 2,Doc.numformat(formulkg,12,2))
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, solToplam, satiryuksekligi, GenToplam - 2,Doc.numformat(toplamkg,12,3))
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")

	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial", 12)
	res = pe1.Print(pe1.GetTop, solMargin + 20, "HAMMADDELERÝ SIRASIYLA TART !")
	res = pe1.UseSet(1)


	res = pe1.StoreSet(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)

	GenArr = Array(15,15,10)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(satirBaslangic - 17, KutuSag, 15, GenArr(0), "Hýz" & vbcrlf & "(rpm)")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 15, GenArr(1), "Süre (s)")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 15, GenArr(2), "Su")

	if S32E_Old.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		MikserTuru = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_Old.EVRAKNO,"MIX_TURU",0)
		ProgramNo = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_Old.EVRAKNO,"MIX_PROG",0)

		res = pe1.SetBold(1)
		res = pe1.Write(UstMargin + 13.5, KutuSag + 1, 5, 40, "Mikser Bilgileri")
		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, KutuSag + 1, 5, 18, "Mikser Türü")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 20, ": " & trim(MikserTuru))

		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, KutuSag + 1, 5, 18, "Program No")
		res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, 5, 20, ": " & trim(ProgramNo))
		res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")

		res = pe1.SetBold(0)
		For i = 1 to 5
			MikserHizi = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_Old.EVRAKNO,"MIX_RPM",i)
			MikserSuresi = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_Old.EVRAKNO,"MIX_SURE",i)
			SuVarYok = Doc.GetUDFValue("SFDC32E",S32E_Old.EVRAKNO,"MIX_SU",i)

			satirPozr = satirBaslangic - 9 + (i - 1) * SatirYuksekligi
			res = pe1.Write(satirPozr, KutuSag, 5, GenArr(0), MikserHizi)
			res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, GenArr(1), MikserSuresi)
			res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 5, GenArr(2), trim(Doc.GetGecoValue("!MIX_SU", "AD", SuVarYok, -1)))
		Next
	End if
	
	for i = 0 to 7
		res = pe1.Line((satirBaslangic-9)+i*satiryuksekligi, Sag2, 0, sagMargin - Sag2)
	next
	sol = KutuSag
	For i = 0 To uBound(GenArr) - 1
		sol = sol + GenArr(i)
		res = pe1.Line(satirBaslangic - 18, sol, 44, 0)
	Next
	res = pe1.Box(satirBaslangic-18, sag2, 44, sagMargin - Sag2)

	res = pe1.PenSize(0.5)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 12.5, KutuSag, pe1.GetBottom  - UstMargin - 12.5, 0)
	res = pe1.Line(UstMargin + 12.5, KutuSag, 0, sagMargin - KutuSag)
	res = pe1.PenSize(0.3)	

	res = pe1.UseSet(1)

	
	
	res = pe1.setalign("LEFT")
	'---------SICAKLIKLAR
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)
	satirsonu1 = satirbaslangic+satiryuksekligi*8
	
'	res = pe1.Box(satirsonu1, solmargin-2, 21, 210)

'	res = pe1.Line(satirsonu1+5, solmargin-2, 0, pe1.GetRight - solMargin + 2)
'	res = pe1.Line(satirsonu1+13, solmargin-2, 0, pe1.GetRight - solMargin + 2)
'	res = pe1.setbold(1)
	
	KutuSol = solmargin+213
	res = pe1.Box(satirsonu1 - 6, KutuSol, 58, sagMargin - KutuSol)
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop + 6, KutuSol, 0, sagMargin - KutuSol)
	res = pe1.Line(KutuAlt - 6, KutuSol, 0, sagMargin - KutuSol)
	AltCizgi = pe1.GetTop

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu1 - 5, KutuSol, satiryuksekligi, sagMargin - KutuSol, "MÝKSER BÝLGÝLERÝ")
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 6, KutuSol + 1, satiryuksekligi, 20, "MÝKSER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, satiryuksekligi, 10, ":")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 5, KutuSol + 1, satiryuksekligi, 20, "PROGRAM")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, satiryuksekligi, 10, ":")
	res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop + 5, KutuSol, 0, sagMargin - KutuSol)
	UstCizgi = pe1.GetTop

	GenArr = Array(7,18,18,14)

	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 1, KutuSol, 15, GenArr(0), "")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 15, GenArr(1), "Hýz (rpm)")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 15, GenArr(2), "Süre (s)")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, 15, GenArr(3), "Su")

	satirAra1 = 5.8
	Basla = pe1.GetTop + 6
	For i = 1 to 5
		res = pe1.Write(Basla + (i - 1) * satirAra1, KutuSol, 5, GenArr(0), i)
		res = pe1.Line(pe1.GetTop - 1, KutuSol, 0, SagMargin - KutuSol)
	Next
	res = pe1.setbold(0)
	
	sol = KutuSol
	Gradesol = KutuSol
	For i = 0 To uBound(GenArr) - 1
		sol = sol + GenArr(i)
		res = pe1.Line(UstCizgi, sol, AltCizgi - UstCizgi, 0)
	Next

	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	res = pe1.Write(KutuAlt - 5, KutuSol + 1, satiryuksekligi, 45,"Numune Miktarý : " & GetStdNumuneMiktari & " kg")

			
	B = solmargin+5
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")


	res = pe1.SetBold(0)


	satirsonu2 = satirsonu1+25
'	Call VardiyaTartimYazdir(pe1, satirsonu2)
	
	satirsonu3 = satirsonu2+29

	Call AmbalajToplamYazdir(pe1, satirsonu3)
	Call GradeYazdir(pe1, satirSonu3)
	
	satirsonu4 = satirsonu3+15
	
'	Call UretimBilgileriYazdir(pe1, satirSonu4)	
	Call KontrolYazdir(pe1)


	evrakno = MMPS10E.EVRAKNO(1)
	kg = Doc.GetUDFValue("MMPS10E",evrakno,"KG",0)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",10)
	If mid(std,1,1) = "*" or trim(std) = "" then
		'res = pe1.Write(satirsonu3+satiryuksekligi*21+3, Sagmargin-38, satiryuksekligi, 4, "X")
	else
		'res = pe1.Write(satirsonu3+satiryuksekligi*21+3, Sagmargin-19, satiryuksekligi, 4, "X")
	End If
	
	'********************************************
	satirsonu5 = satirsonu4+37
	
'	Call DurusFireSebepYazdir(pe1, satirsonu5)
End Sub

Sub Alanlar()
	ReDim Grid(8,7)
	
	Grid(0,0) = "_FORMUL"
	Grid(1,0) = "KAYNAK_AD"
	Grid(2,0) = "R_KAYNAKKODU"
	Grid(3,0) = "RECETENOTU"
	Grid(4,0) = "_R_MIKTART"
	Grid(5,0) = ""
	Grid(6,0) = "_ISI_DAYANIM"
	Grid(7,0) = "_ESKI_HMD"
	Grid(8,0) = "_ESKI_FORMUL"
	
	Grid(0,1) = 18
	Grid(1,1) = 65
	Grid(2,1) = 30
	Grid(3,1) = 23
	Grid(4,1) = 18
	Grid(5,1) = 15
	Grid(6,1) = 14
	Grid(7,1) = 40
	Grid(8,1) = 12
	
	Grid(0,2) = "FORMÜL"
	Grid(1,2) = "HAMMADDE ADI"
	Grid(2,2) = "HAMMADDE" & vbcrlf & "KODU"
	Grid(3,2) = "HAMMADDE PARTÝ NO"
	Grid(4,2) = "MÝKTAR"
	Grid(5,2) = "ÝLAVE"
	Grid(6,2) = "HMD.DY. IS.°C"
	Grid(7,2) = "Hammade Adý"
	Grid(8,2) = "Formül" & vbcrlf & "%"
	
	Grid(0,4) = "N"
	Grid(1,4) = "C"
	Grid(2,4) = "C"
	Grid(3,4) = "C"
	Grid(4,4) = "N"
	Grid(5,4) = "N"
	Grid(6,4) = "N"
	Grid(7,4) = "C"
	Grid(8,4) = "N"
	
	For i = 0 To UBound(Grid,1)
		If Grid(i,4) = "N" Then
			AlanAdi = Grid(i,0)
			Grid(i,6) = MMPS10T.MaxSigDig(AlanAdi)
		End If
	Next
End Sub
Function Basilacak()
	Basilacak = False
	Set M10TH1 = Doc.Select2G("fw4fs", "MMPS10T", "R_KAYNAKTYPE='H'", "", "EVRAKNO", -1)
	if M10TH1.GetRowCount = 0 Then Exit Function
	
	if Doc.Round(M10TH1.R_MIKTART(1), 3) = Doc.Round(MMPS10E.SF_TOPLAMMIKTAR, 3) Then Basilacak = True
End Function
Function GetStdNumuneMiktari()
	kod = MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU(1)
	rows = Doc.SelectEQ("BOMU01E", "MAMULCODE", kod)
	Set Old = Doc.GetTableObject("AU_BOMU01E_SELECT")
	Set B01E = Doc.Select1("asd23g1ggs", "BOMU01E", "MAMULCODE='" & MMPS10E.MAMULSTOKKODU & "'&AKTIF_PASIF='*'", "", -1)
	if B01E.GetRowCount() > 0 Then
		std = Doc.GetUDFValue("BOMU01E", B01E.EVRAKNO,"STD",0)
		if trim(std) <> "" Then
			GetStdNumuneMiktari = 0.15
		Else
			GetStdNumuneMiktari = 0.5
		End if
	Else
		GetStdNumuneMiktari = 0.5
	End if
End Function
Sub UretimBilgileriYazdir(pe1, satirSonu4)	
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",7)
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	GenArr = Array(18,14,14,12,18,22,9,9,11,9,10,18,10,12)
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu4+3, solmargin-2, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(0), "TARÝH")
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu4+1, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(1), "BAÞLAMA")
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu4+5, pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(1), "SAATÝ")
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu4+1, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(2), "BÝTÝÞ")
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu4+5, pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(2), "SAATÝ")
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu4+3, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(3), "VARDÝYA")
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu4+1, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(4), "ÜRETÝM")
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu4+5, pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(4), "MÝKTARI")
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu4+3, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(5), "OPERATÖR")
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu4+3, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(6), "PP")
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu4+3, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(7), "PE")
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu4+3, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(8), "HDPE")
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu4+3, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(9), "PS")
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu4+3, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(10), "KALSÝT")
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu4+1, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(11), "KULLANILMIÞ")
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu4+5, pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(11), "TEM MAD.")
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu4+1, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(12), "DURUÞ")
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu4+5, pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(12), "KODU")
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu4+1, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(13), "DURUÞ")
	res = pe1.Write(satirsonu4+5, pe1.GetLeft, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(13), "SAATÝ")
	sag = pe1.GetRight()
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	sol = solmargin - 2
	For i = 0 to uBound(GenArr) - 1
		sol = sol + GenArr(i)
		res = pe1.Line(satirsonu4, sol, 35, 0)
	Next

	res = pe1.Box(satirsonu4, solmargin-2, 35, sag - solmargin + 3)	
'	KontrolSol = pe1.GetRight + 4
'	KontrolUst = pe1.GetTop
	for i = 1 to 5
		res = pe1.Line((satirsonu4+5)+5*i, solmargin-2, 0, sag - solmargin + 3)
	next
End Sub
Sub VardiyaTartimYazdir(pe1, satirSonu2)
	satirYuk2 = 6.5
	Gen = 20
	res = pe1.Write(satirSonu2 + 1, solMargin - 1, 5, Gen, "1. Vardiya")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuk2, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen, "2. Vardiya")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuk2, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen, "3. Vardiya")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuk2, pe1.GetLeft, 5, Gen, "4. Vardiya")
	sag = pe1.GetRight()
	KolonSayisi = 29
	KolonGenisligi = satirYuk2
	KutuSag = pe1.GetRight + 1 + KolonSayisi * KolonGenisligi
	
	res = pe1.Box(satirSonu2, solMargin - 2, 4 * satirYuk2 + 0.5, KutuSag - solMargin + 2)
	KutuAlt = pe1.GetBottom()
	For i = 1 to KolonSayisi - 1
		res = pe1.Line(satirSonu2, sag + 1 + i * KolonGenisligi, KutuAlt - satirSonu2, 0)
	Next

	For i = 1 to 3
		res = pe1.Line(satirSonu2 + 0.1 + i * satirYuk2, solMargin - 2, 0, KutuSag - solMargin + 2)
	Next
End Sub
Function Barkodgenisligi(Kod)
	Barkodgenisligi = (Len(trim(cstr(Kod))) + 3) * 4.6
End Function
Sub KontrolYazdir(pe1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	GenArr = Array(42,13,13,15)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.Write(KontrolUst + 1, KontrolSol, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(0), "KONTROLLER")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(1), "KABUL")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(2), "RED")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi*3, GenArr(3), "VARDÝYA")
	sag = pe1.GetRight()
	
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
	sol = KontrolSol
	For i = 0 to uBound(GenArr) - 1
		sol = sol + GenArr(i)
		res = pe1.Line(KontrolUst, sol, 35, 0)
	Next

	res = pe1.Box(KontrolUst, KontrolSol, 35, sag - KontrolSol)	


	res = pe1.Write(KontrolUst + 11, KontrolSol + 1, 5, GenArr(0), "RENK TONU")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 5, pe1.GetLeft, 5, GenArr(0), "GRANÜL ÞEKÝL KONTROLU")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 5, pe1.GetLeft, 5, GenArr(0), "GRANÜL HOM. KONTROLU")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + 5, pe1.GetLeft, 5, GenArr(0), "ELEK KONTROLU")
	for i = 1 to 5
		res = pe1.Line(KontrolUst + 5 + 5*i, KontrolSol, 0, sag - KontrolSol)
	next
	res = pe1.SetBold(0)
End Sub
Sub GradeYazdir(pe1, satirsonu3)
	res = pe1.SetBold(1)
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",8)
		
	sol = Gradesol
	Ust = satirsonu3
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + 1, sol + 1, 5, 15, "GRADE")
	Aralik = 7
	KutuEni = 4
	res = pe1.Print(Ust + 1, pe1.GetRight, "A") 
	res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, KutuEni, KutuEni)

	res = pe1.Print(Ust + 1, pe1.GetRight + Aralik, "B") 
	res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, KutuEni, KutuEni)

	res = pe1.Print(Ust + 1, pe1.GetRight + Aralik, "C") 
	res = pe1.Box(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight + 1, KutuEni, KutuEni)
	
	res = pe1.Write(Ust + KutuEni + 3, sol + 1, 5, 30, "B GRADE NEDENÝ :")

	res = pe1.Box(Ust, sol, pe1.GetBottom - Ust, sagMargin - sol)
	KontrolUst = pe1.GetBottom + 2
	KontrolSol = pe1.GetLeft() - 24
End Sub

	res = pe1.SetAlign("RIGHT")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop + satirYuksekligi, sagMargin - 25, 6, 25, "Form No : 39")
End Sub
Sub AmbalajToplamYazdir(pe1, satirSonu3)		
	res = pe1.SelectFont("Arial",9)

	Ust = AltMargin - 24.5
	res = pe1.SetAlign("CENTER")
	GenArr = Array(56,30,30,30,40)
	res = pe1.Write(Ust, solmargin-2, satiryuksekligi, GenArr(0), "AMBALAJLAYAN")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, GenArr(1), "TOPLAM")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, GenArr(2), "ELEK ALTI")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, GenArr(3), "HURDA")
	res = pe1.Write(pe1.GetTop, pe1.GetRight, satiryuksekligi, GenArr(4), "DEPOYA TESLÝM TARÝHÝ")
	sag = pe1.GetRight()
	KutuYuksekligi = 12
	res = pe1.Box(Ust, solmargin-2, KutuYuksekligi, sag - solmargin + 3)	
	'Gradesol = pe1.GetRight + 4

	sol = solmargin - 2
	For i = 0 to uBound(GenArr) - 1
		sol = sol + GenArr(i)
		res = pe1.Line(Ust, sol, KutuYuksekligi, 0)
	Next

	res = pe1.Line(Ust+ 5, solmargin-2, 0, sag - solmargin + 3)
	res = pe1.SetAlign("LEFT")
End Sub

Hangi Tablo için : MMPS10
Ýlave Hangi Uygulamalar için :
